# Requirement: Admin Exchange Rates Page

> **Last Updated**: 2026-10-25

**Created by:** BA
**Date:** 2026-10-19
**Status:** Draft. User decisions recorded 2026-10-19 (§3). **SA requirement review 2026-10-19: CLEARED-WITH-CHANGES (§13)** — BA must apply SA-R1 and SA-R2 (stale baseline, BD-34 / BD-36 reversal) before Dev writes the workplan. **BA applied SA-R1 and SA-R2 on 2026-10-19** (§2.2 and §5.1 rewritten from `origin/main` `4583d21e`; the reversal recorded as BD-55 in the finance requirement, FF-2 superseded by slice 1b). Final status "Approved" needs user or TL sign-off.
**Related:** [BUSINESS_OS_FINANCE_HEALTH_REQUIREMENT.md](/docs/requirements/BUSINESS_OS_FINANCE_HEALTH_REQUIREMENT.md) (BD-12, CUR-3, the Finance page; **BD-55** records the user's 2026-10-19 decision behind this requirement and amends BD-34 / BD-36 for this page; its follow-up FF-2 is superseded by slice 1b here), [ADMIN_LAYOUT_STANDARD_REQUIREMENT.md](/docs/requirements/ADMIN_LAYOUT_STANDARD_REQUIREMENT.md) (closed standard the page follows), [ADMIN_IDENTIFICATION_AND_ACCESS.md](/docs/admin/ADMIN_IDENTIFICATION_AND_ACCESS.md)

## Overview

`/admin/exchange-rates` exists but cannot be trusted or used: all 43 rates were last touched on 2025-11-05, the page is marked redundant and was made read-only (finance BD-34, BD-36), so no admin can update a rate, it still reads both rate tables straight from the browser, and the database still decides who is an admin with `profiles.role`, which the platform has banned as an admin source of truth. This requirement makes the page a **safe, manual, admin-only tool** for keeping a table of exchange rates up to date: an admin opens the page by its URL, edits a rate or switches a currency on or off, and sees how old each rate is. It does **not** convert any money, does **not** produce any USD total and is **not** linked to any customer revenue (user decision D-5). A later slice adds the page as a tab under the Finance admin page, still with no revenue link.

## 2. Current state (verified)

Live facts were read on 2026-10-18 on project `jgccgkyhpwirgknnceoh`. Code facts in §2.2 were re-read on 2026-10-19 from `origin/main` `4583d21e` (SA-R1; the first draft had read a stale local `main`).

### 2.1 Database

| Fact | Detail |
|---|---|
| `public.exchange_rates` | 43 rows. **Every** `last_updated_at` is 2025-11-05. Columns used by code: `id`, `currency_code`, `currency_name`, `currency_symbol`, `rate_to_usd` (units of the currency per 1 USD; `convertToUsd` divides by it), `is_enabled`, `decimal_places`, `last_updated_at`, `created_at`, `updated_by` |
| RLS on `exchange_rates` | On. Policy "Admins can manage exchange rates": `ALL`, role `authenticated`, `USING EXISTS (profiles.id = auth.uid() AND profiles.role = 'admin')`, **no `WITH CHECK`**. Policy "Users can read enabled exchange rates": `SELECT`, `authenticated`, `is_enabled = true` |
| `public.exchange_rate_history` | RLS on. One policy "Admins can read exchange rate history": `SELECT`, same `profiles.role` predicate |
| Table privileges | `UPDATE` granted to both `anon` and `authenticated` on both tables. RLS is the only thing stopping them |
| Migrations | **Neither table has a migration in the repo** (created in the dashboard) |
| History trigger | **Verified live 2026-10-19 (TL).** Trigger `exchange_rate_change_trigger` (`AFTER UPDATE ... FOR EACH ROW`) on `exchange_rates` calls `public.log_exchange_rate_change()`: `SECURITY DEFINER`, plpgsql, **no `SET search_path`**. On UPDATE, when `OLD.rate_to_usd IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.rate_to_usd`, it inserts `(currency_code, old_rate, new_rate, changed_by = NEW.updated_by, changed_at = NOW())` into `public.exchange_rate_history`. Consequences: (a) every rate update must set `updated_by` = the acting admin, or the history row is attributed to the previous editor; (b) enable / disable changes are **not** historised (the audit trail is their record); (c) a same-value save writes no history row. EXECUTE on the function was already revoked from `PUBLIC`, `anon`, `authenticated` by SECDEF slice 5 (`20261051`); its body is captured in `docs/investigations/SECDEF_PROD_ONLY_FUNCTION_DEFINITIONS.md`. `changed_by` → `auth.users` (NO ACTION); both actor columns are already classified by the operator account cleanup (`20261081`) and the purge baseline ("never") |

### 2.3 Why it matters

- `profiles.role` is the banned admin source of truth (CLAUDE.md Security Rules). The source of truth is `admin_users`, SQL predicate `public.is_platform_admin()`, route guard `requireAdmin`, page guard `requireAdminPage`.
- The page's browser reads are direct Supabase calls outside `lib/repositories/` (CLAUDE.md rule 1). The page no longer writes, but the database still lets a signed-in browser session write under the `profiles.role` policy, which is what check 35 measures.
- No admin can update a rate today: the only write path in the product was removed by FF-1a, so the table cannot be kept current.
- Stale rates look current: nothing on the page says "this is a year old".

### 3.1 Proposed CLAUDE.md wording fix (SA / user decide)

CLAUDE.md, Currency & Timezone table, "Never sum money across currencies" row says: "There is no FX rate anywhere in the platform." That is factually wrong: `public.exchange_rates` exists and this work makes it current. Proposed replacement for that sentence:

> "An admin-maintained `exchange_rates` table exists, but no figure may be converted or summed with it."

The rule itself does not change. The edit is a one-line docs change and can ride with slice 1a. Needs SA agreement and user sign-off (CLAUDE.md is the user's file).

✅ **Applied 2026-10-25.** User sign-off, verbatim: "Yes. Change the CLAUDE.md currency rule to: 'An admin-maintained exchange_rates table exists, but no figure may be converted or summed with it.' Docs-only PR with auto-merge." SA had agreed (SQ-6). Code comments that still say "no FX rate anywhere" were left unchanged (out of scope; still true in practice: nothing converts).

### 3.2 Recommendations recorded by BA (not user decisions)

| # | Topic | BA recommendation | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| R-1 | Add a new currency in slice 1 | **No.** Edit and enable / disable only | 43 currencies exist; adding one needs name, symbol and decimal places validation and a duplicate check, which is a second form for no current need. Add later if the user asks |
| R-2 | USD row | Shown, **not editable** (rate fixed at 1, it is the base) | Editing the base would silently re-scale every other row |
| R-3 | Stale threshold | **35 days** since `last_updated_at` | Matches the monthly rule (D-2) with a few days' grace after month-end |
| R-4 | Rate bounds | `rate_to_usd` > 0 and ≤ 10,000,000, at most 6 decimal places | Covers every real currency per USD (the largest are in the tens of thousands, some unofficial rates higher) while refusing typos like a missing decimal point that adds many zeros. SA may tighten |

---

## 4. User stories

- As a platform admin, I want to open `/admin/exchange-rates` by typing its URL, so that I can maintain rates without a menu entry.
- As a platform admin, I want to edit a currency's rate to USD and save it, so that the table reflects this month's rate.
- As a platform admin, I want to switch a currency on or off, so that only currencies we support are offered.
- As a platform admin, I want to see when each rate was last updated, with a clear warning when it is older than about a month, so that I know which rates need attention.
- As a platform admin, I want every change recorded with the old and new rate and who made it, so that I can tell later what the rate was and why it changed.
- As a business user, I never see these rates on any Business OS screen (D-3).

---

## 5. Slices

Slices are small and numbered, reuse existing infrastructure (`requireAdmin`, `requireAdminPage`, the repository pattern, `AuditTrailService`, the admin layout shared components) and add nothing new without a reason.

### 5.1 Slice 1a — Safe manual page (code)

Ships first. It works against today's database policies because all writes move to the server.

| Part | What |
|---|---|
| Repository | `ExchangeRatesRepository` in `lib/repositories/` (use the `new-repository` skill): list all rates, get one by currency code, update a rate, set enabled. Returns `RepositoryResult`. Global, not per-user data, so no `user_id` filter: the repository is used only from the admin route with the service-role client, and the reason is documented in code (CLAUDE.md rule 4 bypass) |
| Route(s) | Under `app/api/admin/exchange-rates/` (use the `new-api-route` skill): `requireAdmin` as the **first statement**; Zod on every input; Pino with `correlationId`; error format per CLAUDE.md. Read all rows; update one rate; set enabled on one row |
| Audit | One non-blocking audit entry per change, recording currency code, old rate → new rate (or old → new enabled), and the admin's user id |
| Page | `app/admin/exchange-rates/page.tsx` today is read-only and reads both tables from the browser (§2.2). Slice 1a **adds** rate edit and enable toggle back, through the route, and moves **both reads** (rates and the newest 50 history rows) to the route's `GET` (SA-R4), so the page holds no Supabase client. The `REDUNDANT (BD-34)` and FF-1a header comments are removed (BD-55 amends BD-34 / BD-36 for this page). Follows the admin layout standard (closed, L-* / LF-*): `AdminPageHeader`, Refresh-only bar with "Read at", shared loading / error / empty states, the standard table constants, `StatusChip` for the stale and enabled states, `formatUtc` for dates (SA-R11) |
| Guards | `redundantPages.source.guard`: Exchange rates leaves G-1, G-3, G-5 and G-6 (the Platform dashboard keeps them). G-6, which bans `fetch(` in the page, is **replaced** by an Exchange rates page guard: no `@/lib/supabaseClient` import, no `.from(`, no `CurrencyService`, no outside host, and the only `fetch(` targets are `/api/admin/exchange-rates`, each rule with a planted control. G-2, G-4 (no in-app link) and the sidebar `UNLISTED` entry **stay** (D-6: URL only). Per SA-R3 |
| Dead code | Nothing to remove in the page: FF-1a already removed its outside fetch, rate edit and toggle. `CurrencyService.updateRate` and `updateRatesFromApi` stay, unwired, with a header note that they must not be wired again (D-1, SQ-1) |
| Logging | The page already logs through Pino (0 `console.*`); it keeps the module name `AdminExchangeRates`. The **9** `console.*` calls in `CurrencyService.ts` are converted to a **browser-safe** logger, because `CurrencySelector` loads the file in the browser (CLAUDE.md rule 3, SA-R12). **User approval for this conversion is treated as given within this scope** (the user asked for the page to be made usable; recorded here so the Dev does not re-ask) |
| Docs | Optional: the §3.1 CLAUDE.md one-line fix, if SA and the user agree |

### 5.2 Slice 1b — Database record and lockdown (migration)

| Part | What |
|---|---|
| Record as-is | One migration that records both tables exactly as they are live (columns, defaults, constraints, indexes, the history trigger and its function if present), written idempotently (`IF NOT EXISTS`) so it is a no-op for structure on prod |
| Replace `profiles.role` | Drop both `profiles.role` policies. Admin reads of `exchange_rate_history` and all writes go through the server route only |
| Revoke browser writes | Revoke `INSERT`, `UPDATE`, `DELETE` on both tables from `anon` and `authenticated` |
| Keep the legacy read | **Keep** `authenticated` `SELECT` of enabled rows on `exchange_rates` (`CurrencySelector`, `currencyHelpers`) |
| History | Verify the history trigger exists and fires on update (§5.4) |
| Scripts | Rollback script and a read-only check script (policies, grants, trigger present, legacy read still works, browser write refused), following the repo's existing migration-checker pattern |
| Apply | **The user applies the prod migration by hand.** The session stops and hands over the scripts. Applied only after slice 1a is deployed |

Whether an admin-read policy on `is_platform_admin()` is still wanted once all admin reads go through the server is an SA call (SQ-2).

### 5.3 Slice 2 — Finance tab

> **Note (2026-10-19, D-7).** The user's page comment 1 ("add it to the settings section of the admin side bar") is **resolved by this slice**: the follow-up answer was "Finance tab only". There is **no Settings sidebar link** and no other sidebar change; D-6, G-2, G-4 and the sidebar `UNLISTED` entry stay. Slice 2 is built **after slice 1c**, so the tab renders the refined view.
>
> **Baseline drift, for the slice 2 workplan.** The paragraph below says the Finance page has no tabs. On `origin/main` `0581637a` it already has three (`app/admin/finance/components/FinanceTabs.tsx`: Overview, Accounts, Plans, selected by `?tab=` through `useAdminFilters`). Slice 2 therefore adds a **fourth tab to an existing tablist**, not tabs from scratch. The Plans tab already owns the URL key `status`, **and the Accounts tab owns `show`** (`FINANCE_FILTER_SPEC.show`: `all` / `active` / `negative`; `financeFilterRepair` resets a non-default `show` whenever the tab is not Accounts), which is why slice 1c's filter uses the key **`currencies`** (SQ-8 ruling, SA-1c-1). BA refreshes this section from `origin/main` before the slice 2 workplan.
>
> **For slice 2 (SA-1c-1).** `FINANCE_FILTER_SPEC.tab` (today `['accounts', 'plans']`) and `financeFilterRepair` must learn the new `exchange-rates` tab, and the exchange-rates view's filter hook runs with `{ active: tab === 'exchange-rates' }`. Slice 2 concern only; nothing in 1c touches `app/admin/finance/`.

> **Refreshed by BA 2026-10-20** from branch `feature/admin-exchange-rates-2-finance-tab` at `c38ad4f4` (the slice 1c.2 tip; 1c.2 is PR #484, in the merge queue). The notes above are kept for the record; the text below **replaces** the 2026-10-19 slice 2 paragraph, which was written when `/admin/finance` had no tabs. User decisions, **not reopened**: placement "Finance tab only" (comment 1, D-7: no Settings link, no new sidebar entry); no linkage to customer revenue (D-5; nothing on finance reads a rate, BD-12, CUR-3); go-ahead on 2026-10-20: "start slice 2 (the Finance tab)".

#### 5.3.1 Finance today (as built)

| File | Today |
|---|---|
| `app/admin/finance/page.tsx` | `'use client'` (authz guard R8), wraps `FinanceView` in `<Suspense>` (`useSearchParams`, Next 14) |
| `app/admin/finance/components/FinanceTabs.tsx` | Page-local APG tablist, manual activation: Left / Right wrap, Home / End, Enter / Space selects, roving `tabIndex`, `aria-controls` / `aria-labelledby`. `FinanceTab = 'overview' \| 'accounts' \| 'plans'`; labels from `financeCopy.ts` (`TAB_OVERVIEW` "Overview", `TAB_ACCOUNTS` "Accounts", `TAB_PLANS` "Plans & subscriptions", `TABS_LABEL` "Finance views"). The finance source guard pins that no file outside `app/admin/finance/` imports it |
| `app/admin/finance/financeFilters.ts` | `FINANCE_FILTER_SPEC`, keys in this order: `window` (`range` / `from` / `to`, alias `preset`), `account` (alias `accountId`), `tab` = `choiceFilter('tab', ['accounts', 'plans'], '')`, `sort`, `group`, `show`, `status`. `FinanceFilters.tab: '' \| 'accounts' \| 'plans'`. `toFinanceQuery` sends `tab` to the finance route **only** for Accounts with no business, so the Plans tab's read is the Overview's. `financeFilterRepair`, in order: (1) "Since it started" without a business → default window; (2) a business with any tab or tab key → drop the tab and its keys (the business wins); (3) tab not Accounts and `sort` / `group` / `show` not at default → reset them; (4) tab not Plans and `status` set → reset it. `FINANCE_CLEAR_PATCH` keeps the tab |

Order: `AdminPageHeader` (h1 "Finance", `asOf` = the window label) → `FinanceKpiStrip` (only when data; placeholders while the first read runs) → `FinanceFilterBar` (window, business picker, Clear, Read at, Refresh) → the content area. **The tablist and every panel render only inside `data && !error`**, and there is no tablist with a business picked. A panel is mounted only while its tab is selected (unmounted on leave), unlike Business OS AI, which keeps a visited tab mounted and hidden. `onSelectTab`: Accounts → `{ tab, status: '' }`; Plans → `{ tab, ...TAB_RESET }`; Overview → `{ tab: '', ...TAB_RESET, status: '' }`, one write each |

#### 5.3.3 The old URL (SQ-4)

SQ-4 ruled: `/admin/exchange-rates` becomes a server `redirect('/admin/finance?tab=exchange-rates')`. Authz guard **R8** (`lib/admin/__tests__/admin-authz-surface.guard.test.ts`) accepts a render entry point under `app/admin/**` that is a client component **or** a server page whose first statement awaits `requireAdminPage()` (R8's self-guarding clause, `guardsItselfFirst`); its exemption lists `R8_PARKED` and `R8_PERMANENT` are **empty and capped at 0** "and it has to stay that way". So a Server Component `page.tsx` that calls `redirect()` **fails R8 unless its first statement awaits `requireAdminPage()`**; none exists under `app/admin`, and SA rejects introducing one here (SQ-17 (d), SA-2-1).

**Ruled (SQ-17, 2026-10-20): option (a), built in slice 2b.** (b), (c) and (d) rejected.

| Option | What | Ruling |
|---|---|---|
| (a) **Ruled** | The Money MK-4 precedent: one temporary redirect in `next.config.js` `redirects()` — `{ source: '/admin/exchange-rates', destination: '/admin/finance?tab=exchange-rates', permanent: false }`, no `has`, with a comment in the existing style — and `app/admin/exchange-rates/page.tsx` is **deleted** (as `/admin/subscriptions` was for the Plans tab) | ✅ R8 untouched. The incoming query is kept and the destination's `tab` wins (`prepareDestination`, Next 14.2.35): `?currencies=all` arrives with it, `?tab=accounts` still lands on Exchange rates. The redirect runs before the admin layout; the destination is gated by it, so a non-admin still gets the layout's refusal. New test `app/admin/finance/__tests__/exchangeRatesRedirect.config.test.ts` in the `subscriptionsRedirect.config.test.ts` shape (shape block + `prepareDestination` block, asserting the parsed query object); `chargeChecksRedirect.config.test.ts` "three redirects in all" becomes four |
| (b) | Keep `page.tsx` as a `'use client'` component that calls `redirect()` from `next/navigation` during render | ❌ Rejected: a client page whose only job is to leave; drops the query unless it adds `useSearchParams` and a Suspense boundary; renders a flash first |
| (c) | A Server Component page + an R8 exemption, cap 0 → 1 | ❌ Rejected: R8 stays at 0 |
| (d) | A self-guarding server page (`await requireAdminPage()` first, then `redirect()`) | ❌ Rejected although R8 accepts it: it would be the first server-rendered page under `app/admin`, a new pattern (CLAUDE.md rule 7) for no gain over (a) |

### 5.4 Month-end rate (D-2): is a new table needed?

A month-end rate is "the rate in force at the end of the month". It can be recovered from `exchange_rate_history` if, and only if, **every** change to `rate_to_usd` writes a history row with the old rate, the new rate and the time. Then the month-end rate for a currency is the newest history row at or before month-end (or the current row, if it has not changed since).

- If slice 1b confirms the trigger exists and writes those three facts: **no new table now.** Slice 3 stays parked until a report needs it.
- If the trigger is missing or incomplete: slice 1b adds it (small, records each change), so rates edited from now on are not lost. Slice 3 stays parked.
- The audit entry from slice 1a (old → new) is a second record either way.
- History before today is whatever exists; the 2025-11-05 values are the only known starting point.

#### 5.5.1 Filter bar (comment 2)

**Pattern:** the shared admin filter bar `AdminFilterBar` (`app/admin/components/layout/AdminFilterBar.tsx`, Admin Layout Standard C-8, §5.5) with the URL state hook `useAdminFilters` + `textFilter` (`app/admin/components/layout/useAdminFilters.ts`, C-7, §5.6). The closest page is **Businesses** (`app/admin/users/usersFilters.ts`, L-4a): one status select kept in the URL, its default (`active`) never written (U-3), an unknown value ignored (U-4), and the search text held by the page and **never** written to the URL (layout standard U-1 exception (1), its D-6 (a)). Field markup follows `app/admin/business-os-invites/components/InviteFilters.tsx`: labelled native `<select>` and `type="search"` input on `ADMIN_FIELD_LABEL` / `ADMIN_FIELD_CONTROL`.

| Control | Spec |
|---|---|
| Show | Native select, label "Show": **Enabled only** (default), Disabled only, All. **In the URL**, like every other admin page filter: key **`currencies`** with values `disabled` and `all`; the default `enabled` is never written, so the plain URL opens Enabled only; any other value is ignored (SQ-8 ruling: `show` is taken by Finance's Accounts tab, SA-1c-1) |
| Search | `type="search"`, label "Search", placeholder "Code or name". Case-insensitive match on the currency code or name. **Held by the page, never written to the URL** (the layout standard's rule for all free text) |
| Clear | Resets Show and Search to their defaults; disabled at the defaults |
| Read at + Refresh | Unchanged from 1a |

- **Where the filter runs:** in the page, over the `GET` payload. The route keeps returning every currency (43 rows); it takes no filter parameter.
- **KPIs count every currency, never the filtered view. BA recommends this.** Under the default filter, "Enabled currencies" counted over the filtered rows would always read "N of N", which says nothing. Businesses hit the same trap (its tile had to say "shown" rather than "total", SA C-15 (a) in L-4a). The history list and the Last change tile ignore the filter too.
- A count line above the table: "Showing N of X currencies". When nothing matches: the shared empty state, "No currencies match these filters.", with Clear available.
- USD is enabled, so it shows under Enabled only and All.
- **A row switched off while "Enabled only" is shown:** BA suggests it stays in place, its chip now "Disabled", until the next Refresh or filter change, so the row and the keyboard focus do not vanish under the admin (SQ-9).
- **Slice 2 compatibility:** the same `ExchangeRatesView` will render inside a Finance tab, where several tabs share one address bar. The Plans tab already uses `status` and the Accounts tab uses `show`, so 1c uses `currencies` (SQ-8). In slice 2 the view's hook takes the `{ active }` option (layout L-9a, F-113), as Business OS AI does.

#### 5.5.3 History capped at 10 (comment 4)

- Today `HISTORY_LIMIT = 50` in `lib/admin/exchangeRates/exchangeRateRules.ts`. It is the one source: `ExchangeRatesRepository` re-exports it as `EXCHANGE_RATE_HISTORY_LIMIT` and uses it in `.limit(...)`. Change it to **10**. It stays fixed and never comes from the caller. The comments that say "50" are updated: route header, view header, `RateHistoryTable` header, repository.
- **Effect on the per-row History button.** Today it filters the loaded rows in the browser ("ILS: 2 of the newest 50"). With 10 rows, most currencies would show nothing. The options for SA (SQ-12):
  - (a) Keep filtering in the browser over the 10. Simplest, but the view is nearly always empty. BA is against it.

#### 5.5.4 Admin name instead of the id (comment 5)

- **Today** `RateHistoryTable` shows `formatShortId(row.changedBy)`, with the full uuid as the hover text.
- **Change:** the `GET` route turns each distinct `changed_by` in the history rows it returns into a display label, **on the server**, in this order:
  1. **The person's name**, from `profiles.full_name` (trimmed, non-empty), through `userProfileRepository.findAdminNamesByIds(ids)`. This is the Audit trail route's pattern (`app/api/admin/audit-trail/route.ts`, the user-names block; ADMIN_BOS_CLEANUP slice 4).
  2. **Otherwise the admin's email**, through `adminUserRepository.findEmailsByUserIds(ids)`. This is the AI model price history pattern (`app/api/admin/model-pricing/history/route.ts`, slice 9, SA SC9-1). It deliberately includes deactivated admins, because history is a permanent record and a former admin should still be named. The archiving route's `adminLabels` uses `listActive()`, which drops former admins, so it is not the pattern here.
  3. **Otherwise "Unknown user"**, which also covers a null `changed_by` (older rows may have one).
- **Lookup failure ≠ no name.** The Audit trail posture: log the failure, still answer 200 with rates and history, leave the labels out, and say so in the body (for example `nameLookup: 'failed'`). The page then shows the short id as today, never "Unknown user" (SQ-13).
- **Wire:** add `changedByLabel: string | null` to `ExchangeRateHistoryWire` (null only when the lookup failed). Whether `changedBy` (the uuid) stays as hover text is part of SQ-13; BA suggests it stays, for tracing.
- **Cost:** at most 10 distinct ids, so one `.in()` read per table, run after the history read.
- **Guards:** `lib/repositories/__tests__/adminReadMethods.guard.test.ts` already lets anything under `app/api/admin/**` call both methods, so the guard needs no edit. But both methods' header comments name their "only caller", so they must be updated to add this route.
- **Privacy.** This is fine because the page is admin-only (`requireAdminPage`) and so is the route (`requireAdmin`). The ids come from the history rows the route itself read, never from the request. The response adds only a display label for the admins who made the changes: no other profile field and no other user's data. Logs carry counts only, never a name or an email (both repository methods already behave that way). D-3 is unchanged: no business ever sees this.

#### 5.5.5 KPIs (comment 6)

The KPIs stay in `app/admin/exchange-rates/components/ExchangeRatesKpis.tsx`, on the existing `KpiStrip` / `KpiTile`. Two tiles, in this order:

| Tile | Spec |
|---|---|
| **Enabled currencies** | "N of X": N = rows with `isEnabled === true` (a database null reads as false), X = all rows. Counts the **whole payload**, not the filtered view (§5.5.1). **USD is counted** (it is a real, enabled currency; the old Stale tile excluded it only because its age could never clear). No status colour |
| **Last rate change** | From the newest history row (the route's order, newest `changed_at` first). Value: "ILS 3.65 → 3.70" (stored strings, shown as stored); old rate null → "ILS set to 3.70". Sub line: "by {label}, {formatUtc(changedAt)}"; the short id when the name lookup failed. No history → "No changes yet" |

- **Do on / off switches count as the "last action"?** No, not from this source. The history table records **rate changes only**: the trigger fires on `rate_to_usd` (§2.1 (b)), and switches are recorded only in the audit trail (`EXCHANGE_RATE_ENABLED_CHANGED`). **BA recommends rate changes only, from history**, with the tile named "Last rate change" so it does not claim more than it shows. Adding switches would need an audit-trail read in the `GET` (SQ-14).
- **The Stale tile is removed.** The per-row "Stale" `StatusChip` stays, unchanged (FR-8: 35 days, measured against `readAt`; USD is never stale, through `isRateStale`). The FR-8 clause about showing a count of stale rates is withdrawn by D-7.
- **Pin test:** `app/admin/components/layout/__tests__/KpiStrip.callers.pin.render.test.tsx` gets **additive cases only** for the new states: two tiles, no history, name lookup failed, old rate null. But it already pins three `ExchangeRatesKpis` cases (`A2 ExchangeRatesKpis loading`, `A2 ExchangeRatesKpis`, `A2 ExchangeRatesKpis none stale`), and replacing the tiles **necessarily changes those three hashes**. That is a named, forced change limited to the Exchange rates cases; every other caller's hash stays unchanged (SQ-15).

#### 5.6.2 The Window control

| Part | Spec |
|---|---|
| Place | **Directly above the history table**, not in the top filter bar (the user: "above the history exchange rate"). One control for both history views: the overall list and the per-currency panel (they are never shown together) |
| Control | The shared `DatePresets`, unchanged: Today, 7 days, 30 days, This month, Custom (From (UTC) / To (UTC), Apply). No `extras`. No business picker (Finance's "Only accounts with a business profile…" note does not apply). `DatePresets.tsx`, `adminWindow.ts` and `adminWindowPresets.ts` are **not edited** |
| Default | **This month** (`{ kind: 'preset', preset: 'this_month' }`). Clicking the pressed preset goes back to This month (the control's own rule) |
| Time zone | UTC, like every admin window (SQ-29) |
| State | Page state of the view, not the address bar (BA default, SQ-25) |
| What it filters | The overall history list and the per-currency History view. **Not** the rates table, **not** the Show / Search filter, **not** either KPI tile |
| Read at / Refresh | Unchanged (the top bar). Refresh re-reads with the window that is applied |

#### 5.6.3 Server-side filtering and the all-time "Last rate change"

- **The window is applied on the server.** The plain `GET` and the per-currency `GET` take the window and return the **newest `HISTORY_LIMIT` (10) changes with `changed_at` inside it**, newest first. They never return the newest 10 overall for the page to trim: a window holding 3 changes shows those 3, and a window holding 40 shows its newest 10.
- **Window bounds:** the inclusive UTC dates `from`..`to` mean `changed_at >= from 00:00:00Z` and `changed_at < (to + 1 day) 00:00:00Z` (SQ-28). The repository applies `.gte` / `.lt` on `changed_at`, the existing order and the fixed limit; the existing index serves it.
- **"Last rate change" stays all-time (D-8).** Today the tile reads `history[0]`; after this slice `history` is windowed, so the plain `GET` returns the all-time newest change **separately**, as a new field `lastChange: ExchangeRateHistoryWire | null` (null when there is no history at all). The tile reads `lastChange`, never `history[0]`. **Flagged for SA (SQ-31):** this amends SA-1c-2 ("Last rate change" = `history[0]`). BA suggestion: a repository read of the newest one row (`limit(1)`, no window), and the label lookup covers its editor too (at most 11 distinct ids, still one `.in()` per source, the same all-or-nothing rule).
- `lastChange` is on the **plain** `GET` only. The per-currency payload (`ExchangeRateHistoryPayload`) does not carry it, so the tile can still never be fed from it (SA-1c-2).
- The rates and the "Enabled currencies" tile are unchanged.

#### 5.6.4 Captions and empty states

Captions **name the period**, so an admin never reads a windowed list as "all history".

| View | Caption (BA wording; Dev may adjust words, not meaning) |
|---|---|
| Overall, list at the cap | "The newest 10 changes this month" / "… today" / "… in the last 7 days" / "… in the last 30 days" / "… from 2026-10-01 to 2026-10-15 (UTC)" |
| Overall, below the cap | "3 changes this month, newest first" (same period wording) |
| Per currency | Prefixed "ILS: ", e.g. "ILS: 2 changes this month, newest first" |
| Empty, overall | "No rate changes in this period." |
| Empty, per currency | "No rate changes for ILS in this period." |

The cap in the caption comes from `HISTORY_LIMIT`, never a literal (SA-1c-4). The period wording comes from one pure helper keyed by the window value, so the two views cannot disagree.

#### 5.6.5 Behaviour details

- A window change re-reads the history for the visible view: the per-currency read when a panel is open, else the plain read (SQ-34).
- After a rate save, the re-reads that already happen (SA Q-3) carry the applied window.
- Leaving the Exchange rates tab and coming back starts again at This month (Finance unmounts hidden panels; SQ-30).
- A refused custom range (From after To) is shown by the control itself and sends nothing.

### 5.7 Slice 1e — Rate sync and suggestions (D-9)

The user's 2026-10-25 request (D-9, verbatim in §3): a **Sync rates** button that reads public rates for every enabled currency, a **Suggested rate** column, a **Gap** column in red or green, and an **Accept suggestion** button on each row. Code facts below were read from `origin/main` `90e739be`. Enabled currencies today: **USD, ILS, EUR, GBP**, so the sync covers **ILS, EUR, GBP** (USD is the base, fixed at 1). D-5 holds: nothing converts or totals money (§5.7.5).

#### 5.7.1 The precedent: the AI model price check, and what 1e reuses

The "AI cost sync" the user names is the **Check provider prices** button on `/admin/system-config` ([AI_MODEL_PRICE_REVIEW_REQUIREMENT.md](/docs/requirements/AI_MODEL_PRICE_REVIEW_REQUIREMENT.md), slices 5, 8, 10; SA rulings RR-1 to RR-8).

| Piece | Price review, as built | What 1e reuses |
|---|---|---|
| Button | `app/admin/system-config/components/ProviderPriceCheck.tsx`: disabled and `aria-busy` while a check runs; a refusal shows in the alert box in the server's own words; after a stored check it re-reads the table (`priceFetchRun.ts`) | **The behaviour**, not the component (it is price-specific): a "Sync rates" button in the rates table header, disabled and busy while running, the server's words on failure |
| Outbound fetch | `lib/ai/pricing-research/fetchSources.ts` `fetchLimited`: URLs are code constants (no SSRF surface), `redirect: 'manual'` (a 3xx is an outcome, never followed), only `User-Agent` and `Accept` sent, an honest User-Agent, a 10 s timer over headers **and** body, a byte cap while streaming, one retry on a network error or 5xx, never throws; outcomes `ok`, `http_<status>`, `timeout`, `blocked`, `too_large`; the body is never logged | **The same shape** for one fixed ECB URL. Whether `fetchLimited` is lifted to a shared helper or copied is SQ-36. RR-8 approved outbound fetching **only for the price review**, so 1e needs its own SA approval (SQ-35) |
| Route | `app/api/admin/model-pricing/fetch-runs/route.ts` `POST`: `requireAdmin` first → strict Zod (an empty object) → 60 s refusal (409) → fetches in parallel → deterministic parse → store pending lines → audit `AI_PRICE_FETCH_RUN` with `logAndFlush` → per-source summary. `GET` returns the last run per source | **The order**, minus the storage step (option (a), §5.7.4): `POST /api/admin/exchange-rates/sync`, `requireAdmin` first, strict empty body, fetch, parse, compute, audit with `logAndFlush`, answer |
| Parse | One pure adapter per source, pinned anchors; a missing anchor fails that source with `layout_changed` and **zero** lines; never "the first number found"; prices as decimal strings, never floats (`decimal.ts`) | **Same rules** for a pure ECB parser. `decimal.ts` has no division, and the per-USD rate needs one (SQ-38) |
| Suggested value and gap | `PriceRowCells.tsx` and `coverageColour.ts`: the server computes the gap (basis points) and the colour; the page prints them through `StatusChip` with words and an icon, and does **no number parsing in the browser** (slice 4 RC-8) | **Same split**: the server computes the suggested rate and the gap; the page prints strings; chips carry words and an icon (A-7: never colour alone) |
| Source cell | Each line stores the human `source_url` and `checked_on` | The suggested cell shows the source, the feed's own rate date and the time it was fetched |
| Per-row decision | `PriceDecisionPanel.tsx`: per-row Apply with a pre-filled, editable reason, bulk Apply, Dismiss; the apply RPC locks the line and checks it is not stale; one audit per line | **The per-row idea only.** Accept calls the **existing** `PATCH /api/admin/exchange-rates/<CODE>`, which already validates, sets `updated_by` and `last_updated_at`, fires the history trigger and writes `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED`. No reason field (the `PATCH` has none), no Dismiss (an unaccepted suggestion is simply left), no bulk (D-9) |
| KPIs | `priceKpis.ts` | Not reused: no new tile (§5.7.7) |
| Storage | Round and line tables, RPCs, migrations the user applied by hand: a price drives every customer charge | Not reused under option (a) (§5.7.4) |

#### 5.7.2 Public source options

Checked on 2026-10-25 by a plain fetch. "Stock market": currencies are not traded on a stock exchange; the closest **free, public, citable** figure is a central bank's daily reference rate. A live market quote is not free or public.

| Source | What it is | Key / account | Updated | ILS? | Licence / attribution | Trade-off |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **ECB euro reference rates, direct** (`https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-daily.xml`) | Official reference rates of the European Central Bank | None | Once per working day (TARGET calendar), around 16:00 CET; none at weekends or on TARGET holidays | **Yes** | Free reuse with the source named ("Source: ECB"); the ECB states they are for information only, not for transactions | Official and stable, tiny XML file (29 currencies). **EUR base**, 4 to 5 significant digits. On 2026-10-25 it carried rate date **2026-10-23** (a Friday): USD 1.1193, ILS 3.4939, GBP 0.83728. BGN is no longer listed |
| Frankfurter (`api.frankfurter.dev`) | Open-source relay of the same ECB data, JSON | None | Same as ECB | Yes | Open source; no stated service level | Accepts `base=USD`, but it computes the cross rate itself and rounds to 5 significant digits (2026-10-25: ILS per USD **3.1215**, where ECB's own figures give **3.121505**). A third party between us and the official source |
| open.er-api.com (ExchangeRate-API "open access") | Commercial aggregator's free tier, USD base | None | Daily | Yes | Its terms require a visible attribution link on the page | Not official, blends sources with no published method, rate-limited. Dev verifies the terms at Step 0 if chosen |
| exchangerate.host | Aggregator | **Now needs an API key** (sign-up) | — | — | — | **Excluded**: a secret to store and rotate |
| Bank of Israel representative rate | Official ILS per USD | None | Working days | ILS only | Official | Good for ILS alone, but a second source for one currency. Not now |
| Live market quote (paid market-data vendors) | Intraday trading prices | **Account, API key and a data licence** | Continuous | Yes | Contract terms | Out of scope: a paid contract and a secret. Free "unofficial" endpoints scraped from finance sites break their terms and are not considered |

**BA recommendation: ECB reference rates, read directly from the ECB** (SQ-37). It is the official source, like RR-1's "official source only" rule for prices; it needs no key; it covers all three currencies we sync; and deriving the per-USD rate ourselves keeps 6 decimals. Frankfurter is the acceptable alternative if SA prefers JSON. **The user confirmed the ECB daily rate on 2026-10-25 (BQ-3, "ECB daily rate (Recommended)")**; the endpoint (ECB direct or Frankfurter) stays SA's (SQ-37).

**From EUR base to per USD.** The ECB gives units per 1 EUR. Our `rate_to_usd` is units per 1 USD, so: rate(X) = ECB(X) ÷ ECB(USD), and rate(EUR) = 1 ÷ ECB(USD), rounded **half-up to 6 decimals**. This is arithmetic on two rates from the same feed, never on an amount of money (§5.7.5, SQ-42). With the 2026-10-23 figures: ILS 3.4939 ÷ 1.1193 = **3.121505**; GBP 0.83728 ÷ 1.1193 = **0.748039**; EUR 1 ÷ 1.1193 = **0.893416**. If the feed has no USD entry (the divisor), the whole sync fails with `layout_changed` and suggests nothing.

**Time and weekends.** The cell always shows the feed's own **rate date** ("ECB rates of 2026-10-23"), so a Sunday sync can never pass Friday's rate off as today's. Before about 16:00 CET the newest file is the previous working day's.

#### 5.7.3 Where the fetch happens

- **Server only.** A new admin route, BA suggests `POST /api/admin/exchange-rates/sync` (SQ-39). `requireAdmin` is the first statement; the body is a strict empty object, so no URL, currency list or rate can be supplied; Node runtime.
- **The server decides what to sync:** it reads the enabled currencies through `ExchangeRatesRepository`, never from the page, and leaves USD out.
- **The fetch** follows the precedent (§5.7.1), copied rather than lifted (SQ-36): one fixed URL constant in `lib/admin/exchangeRates/ecbFeed.ts`, a 10 s timeout over headers and body, a **64 KB** byte cap (the file is about 1.8 KB), accepted content types **`text/xml` and `application/xml`** only (`text/html` and 403 are `blocked`), manual redirects, `cache: 'no-store'`, only `User-Agent` and `Accept` sent, User-Agent **`AgentPilot-RateCheck/1.0`** (admin-triggered exchange rate review; one request per click), one retry on a network error or 5xx, no secrets (SQ-35).
- **The route** (`app/api/admin/exchange-rates/sync/route.ts`) has `requireAdmin` as its **first statement**, then Zod on the body before any read (SQ-39). `runtime = 'nodejs'`, `maxDuration = 30`.
- **The answer** carries the source name, the rate date, `fetchedAt`, and per synced currency: the current rate as read, the suggested rate or a reason it has none, and the gap with its direction (§5.7.5). A source failure is still a 200 with its outcome, like the price check, so the page can say why in plain words; anything unexpected is a 500 with the standard error format.
- **Audit:** a new event, BA name `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` (registered, severity info), written with `logAndFlush`: source, rate date, outcome, and per currency the current and suggested rate. No person's data beyond the acting admin. Under option (a) this is the **durable record of what the feed said** (SQ-47).
- **Logging:** Pino, `createLogger({ module: ... })` with a `correlationId` child; one line per sync (outcome, duration, bytes, currency count). The feed body is never logged.
- **Failure never breaks the page.** The rates table, edit, on / off, filters, history and KPIs keep working. The message sits by the button, for example: "Could not reach the ECB (timed out). No rate was changed. You can still edit a rate by hand."
- **Legacy code:** the sync does not import or wire `CurrencyService.updateRatesFromApi` (SQ-1). That legacy path also wrote floats and applied rates with no review: both are what 1e avoids.

#### 5.7.4 Suggestions: shown only, or stored?

| | (a) Shown only (**BA recommendation**) | (b) Stored |
|---|---|---|
| What it is | The suggestions live in the page until it is reloaded or the tab is left | A new table holding each sync's suggestions with source, rate date and fetched-at |
| Record of what the feed said | The `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` audit entry; the accept itself is audited by `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` (**D-10, BA proposal SQ-50:** by its own `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED`, §5.7.12). The newest successful sync entry also feeds the "Rates last checked" tile (SQ-51) | The table and the audit |
| Build cost | No migration, no new table, no lockdown | A migration the user applies by hand on prod, a 1b-style lockdown, grants, a checker, a repository |
| Cost to the admin | After a reload, click Sync again (about a second) | None |

**Why (a), although the price review stored its results:** the price review stores pending lines because a price drives **every customer charge**, so it needs frozen evidence, a stale check inside an RPC and per-line state. A rate here drives **nothing** (D-5): an accepted suggestion is an ordinary admin edit that the existing route already records. (a) reuses what exists; (b) can be added later if the user wants suggestions to survive a reload or be seen by another admin (SQ-40).

#### 5.7.5 The two new columns, and the gap

**Placement.** After "Rate to USD": **Suggested rate**, then **Gap**. "Accept suggestion" joins the row's Actions. BA default: the two columns appear **after the first sync of the visit**, so the table before a sync is exactly today's (SQ-48).

**Suggested rate cell.** The rate as the server's 6-decimal string, with a second line: "ECB · rates of 2026-10-23 · fetched 09:14 UTC".

| Row | Suggested rate | Gap | Accept |
|---|---|---|---|
| USD | "Base, always 1" | — | None |
| Enabled, in the feed | The rate | The gap | Yes |
| Enabled, **not in the feed** | "No public rate" (never 1, D-4; never borrowed from another source) | — | None |
| Enabled, result not storable (rounds to 0 or exceeds `RATE_MAX`) | "No usable rate" | — | None |
| Disabled | "Not synced (disabled)" | — | None |
| Switched on after the sync | "Sync again to get a rate" | — | None |
| Accepted, or now holds the suggested rate (SA-1e-1 state 1) | The rate | "Accepted" | None |
| **Rate changed since the sync** (the row no longer holds the rate the sync compared against, and not the suggestion either; SA-1e-1 state 3) | The rate | "Rate changed since the sync. Sync again." (no colour) | None |

**Gap: definition (BA default).** Gap = **suggested − current**, shown both as an absolute figure (6 decimals, signed) and as a **% of the current rate** (2 decimals, signed). The server computes it with decimal arithmetic (no float) and the page prints it. "No change" when the two are equal at 6 decimals (`sameRate`). No tolerance band: any difference is coloured (SQ-41). **The direction and colour come from the exact comparison of the two rates (`compareDecimals`), never from the rounded %** (SA, SQ-41): a gap of `+0.000001` reads `+0.00%` and is still red.

**What "losing money" means for a per-USD rate.** `rate_to_usd` is how many units of a currency one US dollar buys. A **higher** suggested rate means the currency got **weaker** against the dollar: any amount held, earned or priced in that currency is worth **fewer dollars** than our table says.

> **Worked example.** Our ILS rate is **3.09**; the suggestion is **3.70** ILS per USD. Gap **+0.610000 (+19.74%)**.
> - ₪1,000 is worth $323.62 at 3.09 but only $270.27 at 3.70. With our old rate, a shekel amount looks about $53 bigger than it really is.
> - The other way round: at 3.09, a $100 price is ₪309; at today's rate it should be ₪370. Asking ₪309 would leave about $16.50 on the table.
> - Either way, money in shekels buys fewer dollars than we think: **red**.
>
> These money figures only explain the colour. **The page shows no amount and converts nothing** (D-5).

**Colour (decided by the user 2026-10-25, BQ-2: "Red = public rate higher (Recommended)"):**

| Case | Colour | Words (with an icon) |
|---|---|---|
| Suggested **higher** than ours | **Red** | "Rate up: currency weaker, we are behind" |
| Suggested **lower** than ours | **Green** | "Rate down: currency stronger, in our favour" |
| Equal at 6 decimals | No colour | "No change" |

This matches the user's words, "red if the current was raised (meaning we are losing money)". It is right when money is **received or priced in the foreign currency**, which is the platform's case (the AI costs are in USD). It would flip only if we **paid** suppliers in the foreign currency. **Confirmed by the user 2026-10-25 (BQ-2).**

**D-5 and the CLAUDE.md rule.** "No figure may be converted or summed with" the rates. The gap compares **two rates of the same currency**, and the EUR-to-USD step divides one feed rate by another. Neither converts or sums an amount of money, and no USD figure appears. The BA reads both as allowed; **SA rules (SQ-42)**. No new code goes under `lib/business-os/`, so `finance.source.guard` is unaffected.

#### 5.7.6 Accept suggestion (per row)

- **Button** on each row that has a usable suggestion, named for screen readers, for example "Accept suggested ILS rate 3.121505".
- **One click saves** through the existing `PATCH /api/admin/exchange-rates/<CODE>` with `{ "field": "rate", "rate": "<suggested string>" }`. This brings the existing rule check (`RATE_PATTERN`, > 0), `updated_by` = the admin, `last_updated_at` = now, the history trigger (when the rate differs) and the `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` audit with the old and new value. The suggested string already meets the rules (rounded half-up to 6 decimals, > 0, at most `RATE_MAX`); a row whose value cannot is never offered a button.
- **Equal suggestion:** the button reads "Confirm (no change)". Saving the same value is already "confirmed for this month" (SA-R7): the date refreshes and no history row is written. This is a quick way to clear a Stale chip.
- **After the save:** the existing re-reads run; the row shows "Accepted" (string equality, no arithmetic in the page) and its Stale chip clears.
- **No "Accept all".** The user asked for row level, and one click per currency keeps each change a deliberate admin act. With three synced currencies it costs three clicks. Revisit if many currencies are enabled.
- **Provenance and concurrency** are SA's: whether the `PATCH` body gains an optional, strict field recording "from sync, ECB, rate date …" in the audit (SQ-43), and whether Accept sends the rate it was compared against, so a rate changed by another admin since the sync is refused with 409 (SQ-44).
- **Audit of each accept (D-10, §5.7.12 B):** the user wants every accepted suggestion tracked as its own activity. BA proposes a distinct `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED` entry, written instead of `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` when the save carries `origin` (SQ-50).

#### 5.7.7 Interaction with existing features

| Feature | Effect |
|---|---|
| Stale chip (FR-8) | Accepting (or confirming) refreshes `last_updated_at`, so the chip clears, as with any save |
| Show / Search filter (FR-14 to FR-16) | The server syncs **enabled** currencies only, whatever the filter shows. Under Disabled only or All, disabled rows read "Not synced (disabled)". The filter never changes what is synced |
| Held rows (SQ-9) | Unchanged. Suggestions are keyed by currency code in page state; they survive Refresh, a save and a filter change, and are gone on reload or on leaving the tab (SQ-30 precedent). **SA-1e-1:** the answer carries, per currency, the `currentRate` the server compared against, and the page picks each row's state by string comparison with `sameRate`: (1) **"Accepted"**, no button, when the code is in the page's accepted set (1e.2; set by a successful Accept or Confirm, cleared by the next sync) or the row now holds the suggested rate (where that differs from the sync's base); else (2) **the gap as served**, with Accept or "Confirm (no change)" (1e.2), when the row still holds the sync's base rate; else (3) **"Rate changed since the sync. Sync again."**, with no gap colour and no button |
| History window (D-8) | An accept that changes the rate writes a history row through the trigger; it shows under the default This month and updates the "Last rate change" tile through the existing re-reads |
| KPIs | ~~**No new tile.**~~ **Amended by D-10 (SQ-51):** a **third tile, "Rates last checked"**, after "Enabled currencies" and "Last rate change", which are unchanged (§5.7.12 C). A "rates out of tolerance" tile is still not recommended: it would need a threshold and a stored sync, and the Gap column already says it row by row. The sync line under the button (source, rate date, fetched at) stays as the status of the current visit's sync |
| Finance tab | The button lives inside the rates view; no file under `app/admin/finance/` changes; sync state is page state, never in the URL (SQ-25 precedent) |
| Guards | `redundantPages` G-6: the folder's literal `fetch(` count goes **3 → 4** (the new `POST` targets `/api/admin/exchange-rates/sync`, which `ALLOWED_FETCH` already accepts). The page still calls only our own route, never an outside host. **D-10:** the page shows a link to the ECB's page but holds no `https://` text: it imports the URL constant from `exchangeRateRules.ts`, so G-6's "outside host" ban stays as it is (SQ-49) |

#### 5.7.8 Rate limits and caching

- The ECB publishes once a day, needs no key and serves a tiny file. One request per click is far below any reasonable limit.
- The button is disabled while a sync runs, and while there is no successful read of the rates yet (SA-1e-2). That is the only guard against repeat clicks.
- **No server cache and no refusal window (SA, SQ-46 rejected).** A per-instance cache on Vercel would disagree between instances and add state for no real saving (about 2 KB from a CDN that already sends `max-age=300`), and there are no stored runs to measure a 60 s window against. Every answer is a fresh fetch with its own `fetchedAt`.
- No scheduled sync, no cron (D-9: a click).

#### 5.7.9 When the feed lacks a currency

"No public rate", no gap, no Accept. Never 1 (D-4), never a guess, never a second source. All three currencies synced today are in the ECB feed; some of the 43 currencies in the table are not, which matters only if they are enabled later.

#### 5.7.10 Proposed split (small numbered slices)

| Part | Delivers | Touches | Cycle |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1e.1** | The Sync button, the server route (fetch, parse, per-USD rate, gap), the audit event, the Suggested rate and Gap columns with every state in §5.7.5. **Read-only: nothing is written to `exchange_rates`** | New route + its tests; new pure parser and rate math (+ fixtures, no network in CI); ~~`exchangeRateRules.ts` (shared types)~~ the new pure `rateSyncRules.ts` (the sync contract, SA §13.8 SQ-52); `ExchangeRatesView`, `ExchangeRatesTable`; `lib/audit/events.ts`; G-6 count | **Full cycle** (a new outbound pattern, SA approval under SQ-35) |
| **1e.2** | Accept suggestion per row, "Confirm (no change)", the Accepted state; SQ-43 / SQ-44 if SA rules them in | `ExchangeRatesTable` / `ExchangeRatesView`; the `PATCH` route only if SQ-43 / SQ-44 add a field | Full cycle if the `PATCH` changes, else TL may choose the short path |

**Order: 1e.1 → 1e.2**, each from `origin/main`, not stacked. Under option (a) neither has a migration or a prod step.

**Amended by D-10 (BA proposal, SQ-52):** the user wants fewer PRs. 1e.1 and 1e.2 stay as two **build steps** in that order, but ship in **one branch and one PR** (`feature/admin-exchange-rates-sync`). The D-10 additions join them: the source line and link (§5.7.12 A) and the "Rates last checked" tile (C) with 1e.1, the accept audit event (B) with 1e.2. The commits inside the PR are grouped by area, as SA recommends (SQ-52); the RM writes the grouping into the workplan before committing. Still no migration and no prod step. One PR means the user sees one larger diff (SA-1e-8 recommends the user sees it).

**Ruled by SA (§13.8 SQ-52):** one branch, one PR, **seven file-disjoint commits** (C1 audit registry; C2 `rateSyncRules.ts`, `rateMath.ts`, `ecbRates.ts` and the fixture; C3 feed, sync route and source guard; C4 last-checked read and the plain `GET`; C5 the `PATCH`; C6 page and pins; C7 docs), each green on its own. SA-1e-8 is now a **gate** for the whole PR: the user sees the full diff and clicks through before any commit.

#### 5.7.11 Unchanged by 1e

- Manual edit and on / off (FR-2, FR-3); USD fixed at 1 (FR-4); D-3 (admin only), D-4, D-5.
- The 1b lockdown, the history trigger, `HISTORY_LIMIT`, the name lookup, the history window, both existing KPI tiles (their values and wording; D-10 adds a third tile beside them).
- `CurrencyService` (unwired, SQ-1), `CurrencySelector`, `currencyHelpers`.
- `app/admin/finance/**`, `lib/business-os/**`, `finance.source.guard`. ~~`KpiStrip.callers.pin` (no KPI change)~~: **D-10 changes it** (the new tile, SQ-51).
- The price review's own code: reused only as SA rules in SQ-36; its tests stay unedited.
- The admin Audit Trail page (`app/admin/audit-trail/**`): no code change. Its Action filter is built from the `AUDIT_EVENTS` catalogue (`buildActionFilterGroups()`, pinned by `filterOptions.guard.test.ts`), so a newly registered event becomes selectable with no UI change (§5.7.12 B).

#### 5.7.12 User additions, 2026-10-25 (D-10)

The user's four points (verbatim in §3, D-10), folded into slice 1e. Code facts read from `feature/admin-exchange-rates-sync` at `origin/main` `90e739be`. The ECB page was fetched on 2026-10-25.

**A. Where the rates come from, with a link (point 1)**

- **The source page.** The ECB's human page for these rates is `https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/policy_and_exchange_rates/euro_reference_exchange_rates/html/index.en.html` (titled "Euro foreign exchange reference rates"; checked 2026-10-25). It is **not** the XML feed the server reads. The page says the rates are published for information only.
- **What the admin sees.** A line beside the **Sync rates** button, always visible (before and after a sync): "Source: European Central Bank (euro foreign exchange reference rates)", where the source name is a link that **opens in a new tab** with `target="_blank"` and `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, and whose accessible name says it opens a new tab. ~~After a sync, the **Suggested rate** column header carries a tooltip with the same source sentence and "Published by the ECB for information only".~~ **Ruled by SA (§13.8 SQ-49 (4)): no header tooltip** (there is no accessible tooltip primitive for a table header; it would be a new pattern). Instead the visible source line carries the note: "Source: " + the label as a link, then `ECB_SOURCE_NOTE` ("Published by the ECB for information only."). The link has `target="_blank"`, `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, an `aria-hidden` `ExternalLink` icon and an `sr-only` " (opens in a new tab)" (BoostBlock precedent). The per-cell second line ("ECB · rates of 2026-10-23 · fetched 09:14 UTC", §5.7.5) is unchanged.
- **Conflict with SA-1e-5 and two guards.** SA-1e-5 ruled "No URL on the page"; G-6 (`redundantPages.source.guard`, `EXCHANGE_BANS`) bans any `https?://` text in a non-test file under `app/admin/exchange-rates/`; the planned `rateSync.source.test` allows exactly one `https://` literal, the feed URL in `ecbFeed.ts`.
- **BA proposal (SQ-49).** Put the page URL and the source name as named constants in `lib/admin/exchangeRates/exchangeRateRules.ts` (pure, outside the page folder, already imported by the page), for example `ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL` and `ECB_SOURCE_LABEL`. The page imports them, so **G-6 stays unedited** (the page holds no `https://` text). The `rateSync.source.test` rule becomes: exactly **two** `https://` literals in `lib/admin/exchangeRates/**`, the feed URL in `ecbFeed.ts` and the page URL in `exchangeRateRules.ts`, each pinned to its exact value. A further rule worth adding: the page URL constant is used only as a link `href`, never passed to `fetch(` (the page still calls only our own route). The feed URL never reaches the browser (`ecbFeed.ts` stays `server-only`).
- This **amends SA-1e-5** if SA agrees.
- **Ruled by SA (§13.8 SQ-49):** accepted, amended. G-6 stays unedited, with no allow-list. The three constants `ECB_SOURCE_LABEL`, `ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL` and `ECB_SOURCE_NOTE` live in the **new** pure module `lib/admin/exchangeRates/rateSyncRules.ts`, **not** in `exchangeRateRules.ts`. `rateSync.source.test` pins exactly two `https://` literals (`ECB_DAILY_URL` in `ecbFeed.ts`, `ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL` in `rateSyncRules.ts`), zero in `exchangeRateRules.ts`; under `app/admin/exchange-rates/**` the page URL appears once, as `href={ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL}`; neither `ECB_DAILY_URL` nor `ecbFeed` is imported under `app/admin/`. SA-1e-5 is amended accordingly.

**B. An audit entry for each accepted rate (point 2)**

- **Today's plan.** Each sync is audited as `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` (SQ-47). An accepted suggestion would be audited as `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED`, with `origin: { kind: 'sync', source: 'ecb', rateDate }` in its details (SQ-43). To find the accepts on the Audit Trail page, an admin would have to open every `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` entry.
- **BA proposal (SQ-50, recommended): a distinct event, `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED`.** Registered in `lib/audit/events.ts` (severity info, `SOC2`, like its siblings) and in `lib/audit/eventAudience.ts` as `'shared'`. The `PATCH` writes it **instead of** `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` when the `rate` save carries `origin` (so one save is still one audit entry). Details: `currencyCode`, `oldRate` (from the route's pre-read), `suggestedRate` (the saved rate), `source`, `rateDate` (as the page reported them in `origin`), and the gap (absolute and %), **computed on the server** by `rateGap(oldRate, suggestedRate)`, never taken from the page. "Confirm (no change)" is an accept too: same event, gap "No change". A manual edit is unchanged (`EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED`).
- **Alternative (b):** keep `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` with `origin` in the details (SQ-43 as ruled). Less to register, but an accept cannot be filtered on its own.
- **Both events show on the Audit Trail page with no UI change.** `app/admin/audit-trail/page.tsx` builds the Action filter from the `AUDIT_EVENTS` catalogue (`buildActionFilterGroups()`; `filterOptions.guard.test.ts` forbids a hand-written option), so `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` and `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED` become selectable once registered.
- **Knock-on:** the `eventAudience` pin moves one more step (219 → **220**, shared 75 → **76**, if SA rules (a)). The history trigger and `updated_by` are unaffected: the save itself is the same.
- **Ruled by SA (§13.8 SQ-50):** (a) accepted, amended. An accepted suggestion writes `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED` **instead of** `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` when the `rate` save carries `origin` (info, `SOC2`, `'shared'`). Its `details` are a superset of the sibling's shape: `{ currencyCode, field: 'rate', oldValue, newValue, unchanged, origin: { kind, source, rateDate }, gap: { gapAbsolute, gapPercent, direction } }`. The BA's `oldRate` / `suggestedRate` become `oldValue` (the pre-read) / `newValue` (the stored value); `gap` is `rateGap(oldValue, newValue)` on the server. **`origin` requires `expectedRate`** (a `.superRefine` on the `rate` branch; `origin` alone is a 400). "Confirm (no change)" writes the same event with `unchanged: true` and direction `none`. A manual edit, a 400, a 404 and a 409 are unchanged. The pin is **220 / bos 60 / shared 76 / agentspilot 84**. `filterOptions.guard.test.ts` and `filterOptions.test.ts` are **not edited**.

**C. KPI tile "Rates last checked" (point 3)**

- **Value:** the date and time of the **last successful** sync, then the ECB rate date it brought, for example "Oct 25, 14:02 UTC · ECB rates of Oct 23". **"Never"** when no successful sync exists. A failed sync does not move the tile (it stays recorded in the audit). Format follows the existing tiles (UTC, sliced from ISO strings, SA-1e-5's determinism rule).
- **It must survive a reload**, but suggestions are not stored (SQ-40, option (a)).
- **BA proposal (SQ-51): read it from the audit, no migration.** The plain `GET /api/admin/exchange-rates` gains a field (BA name `lastSync: { checkedAt, rateDate } | null`), read through a **new** `AuditTrailRepository` method, BA name `findLatestAdminExchangeRateSyncRun(context)`: the newest `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` entry whose details record outcome `ok`, selecting only `created_at` and the rate date. There is no such method today; the precedent is `listAdminPricingSyncRuns` (`AuditTrailRepository.ts` from line 733): an admin read context required, no `.eq('user_id')` (a platform event, the user id is the acting admin; documented in the repository header like the fourth and fifth admin exceptions), a fixed column list, `AUDIT_EVENTS` constants, its only caller behind `requireAdmin`. This changes SA's "no new repository method" note in §13.7.
- **Freshness.** The sync writes its entry with `logAndFlush` (SQ-47), so the row exists before the `POST` answers; a plain `log()` would only queue it. After a successful sync the page shows the new value straight from the sync answer (`fetchedAt`, rate date), and every later `GET` reads it from the audit.
- **Alternative:** a one-row settings table for the last sync, which needs a migration the user applies by hand, a lockdown and a checker. Not recommended.
- **Placement and tests.** The **third** tile, after "Enabled currencies" and "Last rate change" (both unchanged); the loading strip shows three placeholders. This **amends SQ-45** ("no new tile") and changes `KpiStrip.callers.pin`: new cases appended (never synced, synced), the `loading` case re-hashed for three placeholders, and any case whose fixture lacks `lastSync` given one, each with a dated comment line. A failed audit read shows the tile as unavailable, never "Never" (an unknown is not a "no"), and does not fail the `GET`.
- **Ruled by SA (§13.8 SQ-51):** accepted, amended; no migration, no index.
  - **Repository:** `AuditTrailRepository.findLatestAdminExchangeRateSync(context)` (not `…SyncRun`), with the exported column list `ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_COLUMNS = 'created_at, rate_date:details->>rateDate'`; filters `action`, `entity_type = 'exchange_rate'`, `details->>outcome = 'ok'`, newest first, one row. The repository header records it as **the sixth admin exception**. Its only caller is the plain `GET`; the name joins `ADMIN_METHODS` in `adminReadMethods.guard`, in the same commit as its caller.
  - **Three states on the wire**, not `| null`: `LastRateSyncWire = { status: 'ok'; checkedAt; rateDate } | { status: 'none' } | { status: 'unavailable' }`, on `ExchangeRatesReadPayload = ExchangeRatesPayload & { lastSync }`, both in `rateSyncRules.ts` (`ExchangeRatesPayload` itself unchanged). The read is the fourth member of the existing `Promise.all`, through `settleLastSync()`: an error or a throw gives `unavailable`, never a 500. The `?currency=` read never calls it.
  - **After a sync, the sync answer feeds the tile:** a pure `lastSyncFromSync(answer)` gives `ok` with `checkedAt: fetchedAt` when the outcome is `ok`, else `null`. No re-`GET`. The view keeps the visit's value ahead of the `GET`'s, so a later re-read cannot move the tile back. A failed sync leaves the tile unchanged.
  - **Tile wording:** value `formatUtc(checkedAt)`, sub "ECB rates of {rateDate}". **"No sync on record"** for `none` (not "Never": audit archiving can remove an old entry). For `unavailable`, the tile's existing **"Could not check"** status, no value, sub "The audit trail could not be read".

**D. Fewer PRs (point 4)**

- The user wants fewer PRs, with the RM grouping commits as SA recommends, written in the workplan.
- **BA proposal (SQ-52): one PR for all of slice 1e** (§5.7.10 amended), commits grouped by area. A BA starting point for SA:
  1. Audit registry: `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` and `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED` in `events.ts` and `eventAudience.ts`, plus the pin.
  2. Pure rules and maths: `rateMath.ts`, `ecbRates.ts`, the fixture, `exchangeRateRules.ts` types and the two source constants, with their tests.
  3. Server fetch and route: `ecbFeed.ts`, `sync/route.ts`, `rateSync.source.test.ts`.
  4. Last checked read: the new `AuditTrailRepository` method, `lastSync` on the plain `GET`, their tests.
  5. Accept on the server: the `PATCH` `expectedRate`, `origin` and the new event, with tests.
  6. Page: the button, source line and link, the columns and row states, Accept, the third tile, G-6 3 → 4, `KpiStrip.callers.pin`.
- The RM records SA's final grouping in the workplan (`docs/workplans/ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATES_SLICE_1E1_WORKPLAN.md`, or the renamed 1e workplan) before the first commit.
- **Ruled by SA (§13.8 SQ-52):** one branch, one PR, seven file-disjoint commits (C1 to C7, §13.8 commit plan), each required green on its own. The sync contract moves to the new `rateSyncRules.ts` (so `exchangeRateRules.ts` changes for the `PATCH` keys alone and each file sits in one commit). The workplan is renamed `docs/workplans/ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATES_SLICE_1E_WORKPLAN.md`.

### 5.8 Slice 1f — Last check by whom (D-11)

Read from `origin/main` `e0d64fb2` (slice 1e merged, #511).

- **Today.** The "Rates last checked" tile shows when the last successful sync ran and the ECB rate date it brought, but not who ran it. The sync's audit entry already records the acting admin (`sync/route.ts` writes `userId` and `actorId`, both the admin). The tile's read deliberately selects no actor (`AuditTrailRepository.findLatestAdminExchangeRateSync`, header: "no actor, no hash, no email, no ip").
- **Wanted (D-11).** The tile's sub-line names that admin, the way "Last rate change" names its editor: "by Anna Admin · ECB rates of 2026-10-23". The same name rule as the history (FR-19): profile name, else admin email, else "Unknown user"; when the name lookup fails, the short id.
- **After a sync** the tile names the admin who just ran it at once, and a reload shows the same name.
- **Unchanged.** The tile's value (the time), its "No sync on record" and "Could not check" states, the sync itself and its audit entry, the other two tiles, the history.
- **No migration.** Every sync entry since 1e already holds the actor.
- **For SA:** which audit column holds the actor, how the name gets to the tile right after a sync without a new request, and the pins. Ruled in §13.9.

## 6. Functional requirements

| # | Requirement | Slice |
|---|---|---|
| FR-1 | The page lists every currency in `exchange_rates` with code, name, symbol, rate to USD, enabled, and last updated (UTC) | 1a |
| FR-2 | An admin can edit a rate and save it. The save goes through the admin route, never the browser client | 1a |
| FR-3 | An admin can switch a currency on or off. Same route rule | 1a |
| FR-4 | The USD row's rate cannot be edited (R-2) | 1a |
| FR-5 | No currency can be added or deleted in this scope (R-1) | 1a |
| FR-6 | The route refuses: a rate ≤ 0, a rate above the upper bound, more than 6 decimal places, a non-numeric rate, a currency code that is not three upper-case letters or that does not exist | 1a |
| FR-7 | Each saved change writes `last_updated_at` = now and `updated_by` = the admin's user id, and writes one non-blocking audit entry with old and new values | 1a |
| FR-8 | Each row shows its age. A rate older than 35 days shows a clear "Stale" status (text, not colour alone). The header or KPI area shows how many enabled rates are stale (**count withdrawn by D-7 in 1c; the row status stays**, FR-22) | 1a |
| FR-9 | A save failure shows a short message on the page and leaves the previous value showing; it never shows raw internal errors | 1a |
| FR-10 | Only platform admins can read or change rates through the route (`requireAdmin`); everyone else gets the standard refusal | 1a |
| FR-11 | After 1b, the browser roles cannot insert, update or delete either table; signed-in users can still read enabled rows | 1b |
| FR-12 | Exchange rates is a tab on `/admin/finance`; the existing finance view is the default tab and is unchanged | 2a |
| FR-13 | No screen, route or calculation converts money or totals across currencies with these rates (D-5) | All |
| FR-14 | The page has the shared admin filter bar: Show (Enabled only by default, Disabled only, All), Search (code or name), Clear, Read at, Refresh (§5.5.1) | 1c.2 |
| FR-15 | Show is kept in the URL under the key `currencies` (values `disabled` / `all`; the default `enabled` is never written; an unknown value is ignored); the search text is never written to the URL | 1c.2 |
| FR-16 | The filter narrows the rates table only. The KPIs, the history list and the Last change tile always count every currency. A count line says how many currencies are shown; an empty result shows the shared empty state | 1c.2 |
| FR-17 | ~~Country column with flags~~ — **withdrawn: dropped by the user** 2026-10-19 ("skip this requirement, not needed", §5.5.2) | — |
| FR-18 | The history list shows the newest **10** rate changes (fixed, never caller-supplied); the per-currency view follows SQ-12 | 1c.1 |
| FR-19 | "Changed by" shows the admin's name, else their admin email, else "Unknown user", resolved on the server; a failed lookup shows the short id, not "Unknown user" (§5.5.4) | 1c.1 |
| FR-20 | KPI "Enabled currencies": N of X, over all currencies, USD included | 1c.1 |
| FR-21 | KPI "Last rate change": the newest history row as "CODE A → B", with who and when; "No changes yet" when there is none. On / off switches are not counted (SQ-14) | 1c.1 |
| FR-22 | No Stale KPI tile. The per-row Stale status of FR-8 stays unchanged | 1c.1 |
| FR-23 | The Finance tablist has a fourth and last tab, "Exchange rates", at `?tab=exchange-rates`, keyboard-usable like the other three (§5.3.2) | 2a |
| FR-24 | On that tab the Finance KPI strip, the Finance filter bar and the window "as of" line are not shown; the rates view's own KPIs, filter bar and states are; the page has exactly one h1, "Finance" (§5.3.2, SQ-21) | 2a |
| FR-25 | The rates on the tab show whether or not the finance read has finished or failed, with no finance error or loading state on the tab (SQ-19) | 2a |

| FR-27 | No sidebar change: no Settings link and no new item (D-7) | 2a |
| FR-28 | A Window control (the shared `DatePresets`: Today, 7 days, 30 days, This month, Custom with From (UTC) / To (UTC) and Apply) sits directly above the history table; default **This month**; UTC (D-8, §5.6.2) | 1d |
| FR-29 | The history table shows the newest **10** rate changes whose `changed_at` lies **inside the window**, newest first, selected **on the server** (never the newest 10 overall trimmed in the page). Amends FR-18 | 1d |
| FR-30 | The per-currency History view follows the same window, with the same server-side rule | 1d |
| FR-31 | The "Last rate change" tile stays **all-time**: it reads a separate all-time `lastChange` from the plain `GET`, never the windowed list. Amends SA-1c-2 (SQ-31) | 1d |

| FR-34 | The window does not affect the rates table, the Show / Search filter or the "Enabled currencies" tile | 1d |
