### 13.6 SA review — slice 1d

**Reviewed by SA — 2026-10-20.** This is a requirement review of the §1 1d row, D-8, §5.6, FR-28 to FR-34, the §8 slice 1d criteria, the §12 1d rows, and SQ-25 to SQ-34. Code was read from `feature/admin-exchange-rates-1d-history-window` at `origin/main` `930a6cee`.

**Verdict: CLEARED-WITH-CHANGES. Dev builds from the step list below; there is no separate workplan review.** The slice is proportionate. It adds no table, migration, write path, dependency or new fetch site. It reuses the shared Window control and the admin window helpers unedited, and it keeps every Finance file untouched.

Two BA proposals change:
- **SQ-34.** A window change re-reads what Refresh re-reads, not the panel only. Otherwise the overall list behind an open panel goes stale.
- **SQ-32.** No new pin case.

Four gaps the BA did not list are added: SA-1d-1 to SA-1d-4. They cover a request race on the plain read, the caption naming the window the rows were read under, a date range that would otherwise reach Postgres as a 500, and the ordering of a null `changed_at`.

**Not split.** A server-only half would either ship a windowed list with no control and no period caption, or need a temporary all-time default that is then reversed. The slice is one feature over six source files.

**Cycle:** full cycle. The route's query and payload change.

**User decisions, not reopened (D-8):**
- The control has the same five presets, defaults to This month, and shows the newest 10 in the window.
- It filters the history table and the per-currency History view only.
- "Last rate change" stays all-time.

**Verified against the code:**

| Claim | Result |
|---|---|
| `DatePresets` props and rules (§5.6.1) | ✅ `app/admin/components/layout/DatePresets.tsx`: `value`, `defaultValue`, `onChange`, `extras`, `requireBothDates`. A preset applies at once. A second click on the pressed preset calls `onChange(defaultValue)`, so with the default This month pressed it calls `onChange` with an **equal** value (SA-1d-6). "Custom" only opens the row. With `requireBothDates`, Apply stays disabled until both dates are set. Only "From after To" is refused. The type still allows `{ kind: 'custom', from: null }`, so the page's helper must narrow (step 4). **No page imports `DatePresets` directly today.** Every caller goes through `AdminFilterBar`'s `windowControl`. No guard forbids a direct import (`layoutFolder.guard` lists the file and has no import rule for it). See SA-1d-5 |
| `resolveWindow` / `resolveAdminWindow` / `isRealDate`, UTC | ✅ `adminWindow.ts` `resolveWindow(value, now)` maps `preset` through `resolveAdminWindow` and returns `custom` as given. `adminWindowPresets.ts` is UTC throughout (`Date.UTC`, `getUTC*`). `this_month` runs from the 1st to today; `7d` / `30d` include today. `isRealDate` checks the pattern and the round-trip, so it refuses `2026-02-30` and `''`. It **accepts** `0000-01-01` and `9999-12-31` (SA-1d-3). `customRangeProblem` is **not** reusable here: it refuses a `to` after today and anything over 92 days, neither of which this slice wants (SQ-26) |
| Finance owns `range` / `from` / `to` on the tab (SQ-25) | ✅ `FinanceView.tsx:151` `useAdminFilters(FINANCE_FILTER_SPEC)` is unconditional, with no `active` option, so it is mounted on the Exchange rates tab. `financeFilters.ts:116-119`: `window: windowFilter(...)` with alias `preset` → `range`, so it owns `range`, `from`, `to` and reads `preset`. A history window in the URL would be parsed as Finance's window and would change the finance request string. Finance's filter bar is not rendered on the tab (SQ-19 (i)), so the history control is the only group named "Window" on screen |
| Panels unmount (SQ-30) | ✅ As ruled in §13.5 (SQ-20 / SQ-19). `ExchangeRatesView` exists only while its tab is selected, so its page state starts at the default on every visit and on reload |
| Strict query schema | ✅ `exchangeRateRules.ts` `exchangeRatesQuerySchema = z.object({ currency: currencyCodeSchema.optional() }).strict()`. The route refuses a repeated key **before** the parse (`params.getAll(key).length > 1`), then parses `Object.fromEntries(params)`. Adding `.superRefine` turns it into a `ZodEffects`; `safeParse` and `query.data.currency` still work |
| Repository history reads | ✅ `listRecentHistory()` and `listRecentHistoryForCurrency(code)` select `HISTORY_COLUMNS`, `.order('changed_at', { ascending: false }).limit(EXCHANGE_RATE_HISTORY_LIMIT)`. Neither takes a window today. The header promises "No method accepts a caller-supplied filter … or limit" and must be reworded (step 3). `changed_at` is `timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now()` and **nullable** (`20261082` line 116). The trigger always sets `NOW()`, but `ExchangeRateHistoryRow.changed_at` is typed `string` (SA-1d-4). Index `idx_exchange_rate_history_changed_at` exists (line 126) |
| `nameLookup` | ✅ `route.ts` `resolveLabels(records)` takes the ids from `historyEditorIds(records)` (distinct, non-null). It runs both sources in parallel through `settle` (returned and thrown errors are the same failure), and any failure gives `labelHistory(records, { status: 'failed' })`. It returns already-labelled rows, so as written a second list (`lastChange`) would need a second lookup and could disagree with the first. Step 5 splits it into "resolve once" and "label each list" |
| `KpiStrip.callers.pin` (SQ-32) | ✅ Five `ExchangeRatesKpis` cases (lines 294-298, hashes 359-363). The fixtures (`FX_PAYLOAD` and the three spreads, lines 209-217) are typed `ExchangeRatesPayload`, and Jest runs `ts-jest`, so a required `lastChange` **must** be added to them. The tile reads only `lastChange`, so with `lastChange` equal to today's `history[0]` (`null` for no history) the HTML, and so **every hash, is unchanged**. `KpiStrip.tsx` is not touched. With a payload present, `loading` does not swap the tiles for placeholders (line 54), so a window re-read does not flicker the strip |
| `redundantPages` G-6 (SQ-33) | ✅ `` ALLOWED_FETCH = /^\s*(['"`])\/api\/admin\/exchange-rates(['"`/?]\|\$\{)/ `` (guard line 128; the table cell escapes the `\|`). A template literal `` `/api/admin/exchange-rates?${…}` `` passes on the `?` branch. The per-currency site already does. `fetchCount` counts literal `fetch(` sites, so the count stays **3** when both GET sites gain parameters in place. The `\$\{` branch would also pass `` `/api/admin/exchange-rates${suffix}` ``; that form is still forbidden by the SQ-12 ruling (step 6) |
| Held rows vs re-reads (SQ-9, SQ-34) | ✅ `ExchangeRatesView.tsx`: `refresh()` empties the held set **before** its reads, and `load()` empties it on every successful plain read. `load` is `useCallback([])` with **no request counter**, so only the one-currency read has one (`historyRequest`, SA W-3). Two quick window changes could therefore let an older plain answer land last (SA-1d-1) |
| SQ-27 precedent | ✅ The layout-standard pages resolve on the client and send dates: `costsFilters.ts` `costsRouteSearch` and `activityFilters.ts` `activityRouteSearch` send `from` / `to` as whole dates. The AI activity route (`app/api/admin/business-os/ai-activity/route.ts`) takes `from` / `to` `YYYY-MM-DD`, strict. Audit trail and Analytics also send resolved bounds. Finance is the exception: its route takes `preset` and re-resolves with its own clock, because its money aggregates must not trust a client clock. A `lib/` module importing the pure `adminWindowPresets.ts` has precedent too (`lib/business-os/finance/dueDates.ts:20`) |
| Forced test edits (not listed by BA) | ⚠️ Exact-URL matchers break once the GETs carry parameters: `exchangeRates.render.test.tsx` lines 171, 347, 565, 580, and `exchangeRatesFilters.render.test.tsx:143`. Caption assertions using `toHaveTextContent` (a substring match) break on the new period wording: `exchangeRates.render.test.tsx` lines 444, 451, 459, 462, 475, 511, 589. The route-test repository mock drops arguments (`listRecentHistory: () => listRecentHistory()`, line 54). `app/admin/finance/__tests__/exchangeRatesTab.render.test.tsx` matches with `startsWith(RATES_PATH)`, types its payload `unknown` and asserts only that `kpi-last-rate-change` exists, so it stays green **unedited** |

#### Rulings

| # | Ruling |
|---|---|
| SQ-25 | **Accepted: page state only.** `useState<AdminWindowValue>` in `ExchangeRatesView`, default `HISTORY_DEFAULT_WINDOW` (step 4). No URL key. No edit to `FINANCE_FILTER_SPEC`, `financeFilterRepair`, `useAdminFilters` or `EXCHANGE_RATES_FILTER_SPEC`. `financeRequest.pin` is green and unedited |
| SQ-26 | **Accepted, with one addition (SA-1d-3).** `exchangeRatesQuerySchema` stays `.strict()`, and the repeated-key refusal stays first. It gains `from` and `to`, each `z.string().refine(isRealDate)` and inside the SA-1d-3 range. A `superRefine` requires **both or neither**, and `from ≤ to` (a string compare, after both are real dates). Both absent → the server default, `resolveAdminWindow(HISTORY_DEFAULT_PRESET, new Date())`, so every existing no-query route test keeps its meaning. `to` after today is allowed. **No 92-day cap**: the read is 10 rows on an indexed column, not an aggregate. The same keys apply to the plain read and to `currency` + `from` + `to`. The limit never comes from the request |
| SQ-27 | **Accepted: the page resolves and sends whole dates.** This is consistent with Costs, AI activity, Audit trail and Analytics (verified above). Finance's re-resolution is the money-aggregate exception, not the repo's rule for a list. Presets are resolved **at each read**, with `new Date()` at call time: Refresh and the save re-reads re-resolve, so "today" moves with the clock. The server converts the dates to instants (SQ-28). It does not trust them for anything else and has no preset key |
| SQ-28 | **Accepted.** Half-open bounds on UTC midnights: `changed_at >= {from}T00:00:00.000Z` and `changed_at < {to + 1 day}T00:00:00.000Z`. They are built by one pure helper in `exchangeRateRules.ts` (step 2) using `Date.UTC` arithmetic, never local time and never a `23:59:59` literal. `DatePresets` gets `requireBothDates` |
| SQ-29 | **Accepted: UTC**, with the shared labels "From (UTC)" / "To (UTC)" |
| SQ-30 | **Accepted, no code.** Covered by an unmount / remount render test in the rates suite. No Finance test is edited |
| SQ-31 | **Accepted, with a separate method.** `ExchangeRatesPayload` gains `lastChange: ExchangeRateHistoryWire \| null`, on the plain read only. `ExchangeRateHistoryPayload` does not gain it. The repository gets a new `findLatestChange()`: same columns, no window, newest first, `limit(1)`. It is called only on the plain read, in parallel with the other two reads. A failure gives the existing 500, logged `failed: 'lastChange'`. **SA-1c-2 is amended:** "Last rate change" = `payload.lastChange`, never `history[0]`. The label lookup runs **once** over the editors of the windowed rows plus `lastChange` (≤ 11 ids), with the same all-or-nothing rule across both lists (step 5) |
| SQ-32 | **Hashes unchanged, accepted. The additive case is rejected.** The "last change outside the window" case would render byte-identical HTML to `A2 ExchangeRatesKpis two tiles`, because the tile no longer reads `history`, so it adds no coverage of the shared component. The fixtures gain `lastChange` (step 7). `EXPECTED` is untouched. The header gains one line: "Amended by Exchange rates 1d: fixtures gain `lastChange`; no case or hash changed." The "outside the window" behaviour is proven by the view render test (step 8, test 5) |
| SQ-33 | **Accepted: the count stays 3.** Both GET sites stay literal `fetch(` calls whose first argument is a template literal starting `` `/api/admin/exchange-rates? ``. No variable URL, no folded site, no `${suffix}` directly after the path. No guard edit |
| SQ-34 | **Rejected as proposed: a window change runs Refresh's reads.** That means the plain read, plus the open panel's read when a panel is open. With only the panel re-read, the overall `payload.history` stays on the old window, and "Show all currencies" (which reads nothing) would show it under the new window. Running Refresh's reads also empties the held set first. That is the existing SQ-9 rule ("Refresh, every successful read"), and the rates are re-read in the same step, so the table is current. This is recorded for the user's diff review, not a blocker. A history-only read stays rejected (a fourth `fetch(` and a new payload shape) |

#### Additional rulings

| # | Topic | Ruling |
|---|---|---|
| SA-1d-1 | Plain-read race | `load` gets a request counter like `historyRequest` (SA W-3). Every plain read takes a number, and only the newest may write `payload`, the shown window, `error`, `refreshNotice`, the held set or `loading`. Unmount retires it too. A window change while a read is in flight then cannot be overwritten by the older answer |
| SA-1d-2 | Captions name the rows' window | Each answer records the window it was **read under**. The plain read stores it beside `payload`, and `CurrencyHistory` gains `window`. Captions and empty messages use that recorded window, never the control's current value. After a failed window re-read, the rows on screen keep their own period's wording. While a re-read runs, the existing `aria-busy` marks the list |
| SA-1d-3 | Date range | `from` and `to` must also lie in `2000-01-01`..`2999-12-31` (a string compare after `isRealDate`), else 400. Without this, `0000-01-01` (no year 0 in Postgres) or `9999-12-31` (+1 day gives the extended-year ISO `+010000-…`) passes Zod and fails in the database as a 500. Constants `HISTORY_WINDOW_MIN_DATE` / `HISTORY_WINDOW_MAX_DATE` go in `exchangeRateRules.ts` |
| SA-1d-4 | Null `changed_at` | `findLatestChange` orders `{ ascending: false, nullsFirst: false }`, so a row with no time can never be the tile's "last change". The windowed reads exclude nulls by the comparison itself. `listRecentHistory*` keep their existing order call |
| SA-1d-5 | Using `DatePresets` outside `AdminFilterBar` | **Approved as reuse, not a new pattern** (this is the CLAUDE.md rule 7 review). A second `AdminFilterBar` would add a second `role="search"` landmark named "Filters" to the page. The control is rendered **inside `RateHistoryTable`**, between its heading row ("Recent rate changes" and the caption) and its body. It is shown in every state (loading, error, empty, rows), so an admin can always widen an empty window. The two `RateHistoryTable` instances sit in the same slot of the view's ternary, so React keeps one control across opening and closing a panel. No `extras`. No edit to any file in `app/admin/components/layout/` |
| SA-1d-6 | No-op change | A window change whose value `windowEquals` the applied one reads nothing (this covers the second click on the pressed This month) |
| SA-1d-7 | §8 criterion 2 ("a route test proves the newest 10 inside the window") | With a mocked repository, a route test cannot prove a database selection. The proof is in three parts: (a) the repository unit test pins `.gte`, `.lt`, `.order(desc)` and `.limit(10)` on **one** builder chain; (b) the route test pins that the request's dates reach the repository as the SQ-28 instants; (c) a view test shows the page renders exactly the rows it receives, with no trimming. BA rewords the criterion to this; it is a record edit and does not gate Dev |
| SA-1d-8 | Skills and domains | `new-api-route` checklist for the changed handler: `requireAdmin` first, Zod before any read, correlationId, error format. `new-repository` checklist for `findLatestChange` and the windowed reads: `RepositoryResult`, never throws, method logger, counts only. The documented no-`user_id` exemption stays and covers the new method. No entitlements import (type-only included), no LLM call, no cron, no caller-supplied tenant id: `business-os-entitlements`, `bos-llm-call-standards`, `durable-queue-drain` and `tenant-isolation-guard` are not triggered. Schema: `changed_at` is verified from the `20261082` record. No `console.*` in any touched file (0 today in all six), and the new files stay at 0 |
| SA-1d-9 | Logs and privacy | The route may log the window dates at `debug` (not personal data). It never logs a label, name or email (SA-1c-7). The existing logger-spy test is extended to the `lastChange` label |

#### Required changes

**BA (record edits; they do not gate Dev):**
1. §5.6.5 first bullet and SQ-34 consequence: a window change runs Refresh's reads.
2. §8 slice 1d criterion 2 per SA-1d-7; criterion 8 adds the SA-1d-3 range.
3. §8 KPI pin criterion: "no hash moves, no new case".
4. §12: add `app/admin/exchange-rates/historyWindow.ts` (new) and the forced test edits from the table above. The `RateHistoryTable` row adds "renders the Window control (SA-1d-5)".

**Dev, build (the step list is the workplan; record deviations as DV-n in a short workplan MD the TL opens for QA):**

0. **Branch and baseline.** Work in this worktree, from `origin/main` `930a6cee` or newer. Run the narrow Jest set (step 10) once before editing and record the counts.
1. **Rules: the query** (`lib/admin/exchangeRates/exchangeRateRules.ts`).
   - Import `isRealDate`, `resolveAdminWindow` and `type AdminWindowPreset` from `@/app/admin/components/adminWindowPresets`.
   - Add `HISTORY_DEFAULT_PRESET = 'this_month' satisfies AdminWindowPreset`, and the two SA-1d-3 constants.
   - Rewrite `exchangeRatesQuerySchema` per SQ-26: `currency`, `from`, `to`, all optional, `.strict()`, plus the `superRefine` for both-or-neither and `from ≤ to`.
   - Export `type HistoryWindowDates = { from: string; to: string }`.
   - Update the schema's doc comment. `HISTORY_LIMIT` stays 10.
2. **Rules: the bounds and the payload.**
   - Add `historyWindowBounds(dates: HistoryWindowDates): { fromInclusive: string; toExclusive: string }` per SQ-28, pure, using `Date.UTC`.
   - Add `defaultHistoryWindowDates(now: Date): HistoryWindowDates` = `resolveAdminWindow(HISTORY_DEFAULT_PRESET, now)`.
   - `ExchangeRatesPayload` gains `lastChange: ExchangeRateHistoryWire | null`, with a doc comment: all-time, plain read only, SA-1c-2 as amended.
3. **Repository** (`lib/repositories/ExchangeRatesRepository.ts`).
   - `listRecentHistory(window: { fromInclusive: string; toExclusive: string })` and `listRecentHistoryForCurrency(code, window)` add `.gte('changed_at', window.fromInclusive).lt('changed_at', window.toExclusive)` before the existing order and limit.
   - New `findLatestChange(): Promise<RepositoryResult<ExchangeRateHistoryRow | null>>` per SQ-31 and SA-1d-4: `.limit(1)`, then `data[0] ?? null`.
   - Rewrite the header line "No method accepts a caller-supplied filter…" to name the window: two instants the route built from Zod-validated dates.
4. **Page helper, new pure file** `app/admin/exchange-rates/historyWindow.ts` (no React, no fetch; not added to the `layoutFolder` registry, like `changedByText.ts`).
   - `HISTORY_DEFAULT_WINDOW: AdminWindowValue = { kind: 'preset', preset: HISTORY_DEFAULT_PRESET }`.
   - `historyWindowDates(value, now): HistoryWindowDates`: `resolveWindow`, and anything that is not two non-null dates falls back to the default. This is the `costsFilters.datesOf` shape.
   - `historyWindowParams(value, now): URLSearchParams` holding `from` and `to`.
   - `historyPeriodText(value)`: `today` → "today", `7d` → "in the last 7 days", `30d` → "in the last 30 days", `this_month` → "this month", custom → "from A to B (UTC)", anything else → the default's text.
5. **Route** (`app/api/admin/exchange-rates/route.ts`).
   - Dates come from the query, or `defaultHistoryWindowDates(new Date())` when absent, then `historyWindowBounds`.
   - Plain read: `Promise.all([listAll(), listRecentHistory(bounds), findLatestChange()])`, each failure named (`rates` / `history` / `lastChange`).
   - Per-currency read: `listRecentHistoryForCurrency(code, bounds)`, no `findLatestChange`.
   - Split `resolveLabels` into `resolveLookup(records): Promise<{ lookup: EditorLookup; nameLookup: NameLookupStatus }>`, run once over the window rows plus `lastChange`. Then call `labelHistory(rows, lookup)`, and `labelHistory([latest], lookup)[0]` for `lastChange`. Its contract is unchanged (never throws, logs counts only).
   - Rewrite the route header: the query keys, the default window, `lastChange`, and the label lookup over both lists.
6. **View** (`ExchangeRatesView.tsx`).
   - `historyWindow` state and a `historyWindowRef`, both set **in the change handler**, never during render and never in an effect. Do not name a variable `window`.
   - `load` and `loadCurrencyHistory` read the ref at call time and send `` fetch(`/api/admin/exchange-rates?${historyWindowParams(…)}`, …) `` and `` fetch(`/api/admin/exchange-rates?currency=${encodeURIComponent(code)}&${historyWindowParams(…)}`, …) ``.
   - Plain-read counter per SA-1d-1; shown windows per SA-1d-2.
   - `changeHistoryWindow(next)`: SA-1d-6 no-op, else set state and ref, then `refresh()` (SQ-34).
   - Pass `window`, `defaultWindow` and `onWindowChange` plus the rows' window to `RateHistoryTable`.
   - Update header comment lines 17-24: the GETs carry `from` / `to`; a window change runs Refresh's reads.
7. **Presentational.**
   - `RateHistoryTable.tsx` renders `<DatePresets … requireBothDates />` per SA-1d-5.
   - `historyCaption(count, currencyCode, period)`: at the cap, "The newest {HISTORY_LIMIT} changes {period}"; otherwise "{n} change(s) {period}, newest first"; the "{CODE}: " prefix kept.
   - Empty messages: "No rate changes in this period." / "No rate changes for {CODE} in this period." (FR-32, FR-33). Update its header comment.
   - `ExchangeRatesKpis.tsx`: `const last = payload.lastChange;`, and its header comment says all-time, from `lastChange`.
   - `KpiStrip.callers.pin`: fixtures gain `lastChange`. `FX_PAYLOAD` → `FX_CHANGE`; no history → `null`; lookup failed → the same `changedByLabel: null` row; old rate null → the `oldRate: null` row. Add one header line. Show the `git diff` of that file in the handover: no hash or case line moves.
8. **Tests, new and amended.**
   - `exchangeRateRules.test.ts`:
     - Schema: none, `currency` only, `from` + `to`, all three, `from = to` → ok. Only `from`, only `to`, reversed, `2026-02-30`, `''`, `2026-1-01`, `1999-12-31`, `3000-01-01`, an unknown `limit` → refused. A future `to` → ok.
     - `historyWindowBounds`: 10-01..10-20 → `2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z` / `2026-10-21T00:00:00.000Z`, plus month end, year end, `2028-02-28` → 02-29, `2028-02-29` → 03-01, `2027-02-28` → 03-01.
   - New `app/admin/exchange-rates/__tests__/historyWindow.test.ts`: every preset at a fixed `now`, including `2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z` and `2026-10-31T23:59:59.999Z`; custom; the fallback; every `historyPeriodText`.
   - `ExchangeRatesRepository.test.ts`:
     - Both windowed reads pin `gte` / `lt` / `eq` (per currency) / `order` desc / `limit(10)` on one chain.
     - `findLatestChange`: no `gte` / `lt`, `nullsFirst: false`, `limit(1)`, a row or `null`, a returned error, a thrown error.
   - `route.test.ts`:
     - Forward mock arguments; mock `findLatestChange` in `beforeEach`.
     - The window reaches the repository as instants. No query gives This month at a pinned clock (`jest.useFakeTimers({ now })` in that test only, then `useRealTimers`).
     - `lastChange` is labelled, including an editor who appears only in `lastChange`: each source is called once with the union of ids.
     - A lookup failure nulls the `lastChange` label too.
     - A `findLatestChange` error gives a 500 with `failed: 'lastChange'`.
     - The per-currency body has no `lastChange` key and `findLatestChange` is not called.
     - 400 with no repository call: one-sided, reversed, bad date, out of range, repeated `from`, `limit=50`.
     - Not admin → refused before any read.
     - The logger spy finds no label value.
   - Rates render suites:
     - Fix the forced URL matchers by comparing `pathname` plus `searchParams`.
     - Rewrite the forced caption assertions to the period wording.
     - Fixtures gain `lastChange`.
   - New `describe('slice 1d')` in `exchangeRates.render.test.tsx`:
     1. One group named "Window", with This month pressed; the first GET carries this month's `from` / `to` (computed in the test with the helper); caption names "this month".
     2. 7 days → one plain GET with those dates; no `replaceState` call and `location.search` unchanged; caption "in the last 7 days".

No migration, `package.json` or lockfile change. The `[currencyCode]/route.ts` PATCH is untouched.

### 13.7 SA review: slice 1e

**Reviewed by SA — 2026-10-25.** This is a requirement review of the §1 1e row, D-9, §5.7, FR-35 to FR-49, the 1e NFR line, the §8 slice 1e criteria, BQ-2 / BQ-3 and the §12 1e rows. Code was read from `feature/admin-exchange-rates-sync` at `origin/main` `90e739be`. The ECB endpoint was probed once on 2026-10-25.

**Verdict: CLEARED-WITH-CHANGES. Dev builds from the step lists below; there is no separate workplan review.** The slice is proportionate. It has no migration, no table, no new dependency, no new repository method and no write path in 1e.1. It reuses the existing `PATCH` for the accept. The one new thing is a second fixed outbound URL, approved here under RR-8's shape (SQ-35).

Three BA proposals change:
- **SQ-36.** Copy the fetch shape; do not lift it.
- **SQ-46.** No server cache.
- **SQ-38.** Reuse the pure `decimal.ts` primitives, and add only the division.

Six gaps the BA did not list are added as SA-1e-1 to SA-1e-6. The biggest is SA-1e-1: a gap computed against a rate that has since been edited.

**Split confirmed:** 1e.1, then 1e.2, each from `origin/main`, not stacked. 1e.1 is read-only and ships alone. 1e.2 changes the `PATCH` (SQ-43, SQ-44), so it is a full cycle too.

**User decisions, not reopened (D-9, BQ-2, BQ-3):**
- A sync button reads public rates for the enabled currencies.
- A Suggested rate column, and a per-row Accept suggestion. No auto-apply, no "Accept all".
- Gap: red when the public rate is above ours, green when below, no colour when equal.
- Source: the ECB daily reference rate.

**Verified against the code:**

| Claim | Result |
|---|---|
| `fetchLimited` shape (§5.7.1) | ✅ `lib/ai/pricing-research/fetchSources.ts`. Facts: `import 'server-only'`; code-constant URLs; `redirect: 'manual'`, `cache: 'no-store'`, only `User-Agent` and `Accept`; a 10 s timer over headers and body; a `content-length` pre-check, then a streaming byte cap; 403 and `text/html` → `blocked`; an unlisted content type → `layout_changed`; one retry on `network_error` or 5xx; never throws. It is **typed to the price review**: `PriceSource.id: AdapterProvider \| 'litellm'`, and it uses the fixed `PRICE_CHECK_USER_AGENT` |
| The price review's own guard | ⚠️ `fetchSources.source.test.ts` "one outbound door (SC-9)" scans every `.ts` file in `lib/ai/pricing-research/` plus the fetch route. It pins `fetch(` to **exactly 1** in `fetchSources.ts`, and pins that file's full URL literals to the five price constants. Lifting the core out would make that file's count 0, so the guard would have to be edited. That breaks the BA's own condition ("tests stay unedited") (SQ-36) |
| RR-8 | ✅ "Outbound fetching of third-party content from a serverless route is approved **only in this shape and for this feature** … Any reuse elsewhere needs its own SA review." The slice 10 ruling (O-7) is the precedent for approving one more URL under the same shape. **This review is the RR-8 review for 1e.** It is a reuse of an approved pattern, not a new one under CLAUDE.md rule 7 |
| `fetch-runs` route order | ✅ `correlationId` → `requireAdmin` → body read as text, so an empty body is `{}` → strict `z.object({}).strict()` → 60 s refusal (stored runs) → parallel fetch via `allSettled` + `settledToResult` → parse → store → `logAndFlush` → summary. `runtime = 'nodejs'`, `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`, `maxDuration = 60`. A source failure is a 200 with its outcome. 1e keeps this order without the refusal and the store |
| ECB endpoint (SQ-37) | ✅ Probed 2026-10-25: `GET https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-daily.xml` → **200 directly** (no redirect, so `redirect: 'manual'` is safe), `Content-Type: text/xml`, chunked (no `content-length`, so the streaming cap is the one that applies), `cache-control: max-age=300`, about 1.8 KB. The body is the `gesmes:Envelope`: `<gesmes:name>European Central Bank</gesmes:name>`, then one `<Cube time='2026-10-23'>` holding `<Cube currency='USD' rate='1.1193'/>` lines. Single quotes, tab-indented, no backslash. USD 1.1193, GBP 0.83728, ILS 3.4939 match §5.7.2 |
| Worked values (§5.7.2, §8) | ✅ 3.4939 ÷ 1.1193 = 3.12150451… → **3.121505**. The 7th decimal is a 5, so this case exercises half-up. 0.83728 ÷ 1.1193 = 0.74803895… → **0.748039**. 1 ÷ 1.1193 = 0.89341552… → **0.893416**. (3.70 − 3.09) ÷ 3.09 = 19.7411% → **+19.74%** |
| BigInt (SQ-38) | ✅ `tsconfig.json`: `target: "ES2017"`, `lib: ["dom", "dom.iterable", "esnext"]`. The `bigint` type and `BigInt(...)` calls are available. **BigInt literals (`10n`) are a TS2737 error below ES2020**, and Jest runs `ts-jest` on this tsconfig, so they would fail there too. `decimal.ts` already follows this rule: it uses `BigInt(10)` and `BigInt(10) ** BigInt(n)`, never `10n`. `decimal.ts` is pure (no I/O, no `server-only`), exports `parseDecimal`, `compareDecimals`, `subtractDecimals`, `absDecimal` and `signOfDecimal`, has **no division**, and is already imported outside its folder (`lib/repositories/types.ts`). `incrementDigits` in `exchangeRateRules.ts` is private string arithmetic, used by `roundRateForDisplay` |
| Route path (SQ-39) | ✅ `app/api/admin/exchange-rates/` holds `route.ts` (GET) and `[currencyCode]/route.ts` (PATCH only). In the App Router a static segment (`sync/`) wins over a dynamic sibling (`[currencyCode]/`), and `sync` (4 letters, lower case) can never pass `CURRENCY_CODE_PATTERN` `^[A-Z]{3}$`. One side effect: `PATCH /api/admin/exchange-rates/sync` changes from 400 ("Invalid currency code") to 405 (the static route has no PATCH). That is harmless; no test sends it (grep) (SA-1e-6) |
| Admin authz surface guard | ✅ `lib/admin/__tests__/admin-authz-surface.guard.test.ts` checks by shape, repo-wide, and has no handler-count pin. A new `route.ts` under `app/api/admin/**` passes if its handler calls `requireAdmin(`. CLAUDE.md requires that call to be the **first statement**. The `[currencyCode]` PATCH follows that; `fetch-runs` computes `correlationId` first. 1e follows the PATCH |
| G-6 (`redundantPages.source.guard`) | ✅ `` ALLOWED_FETCH = /^\s*(['"`])\/api\/admin\/exchange-rates(['"`/?]\|\$\{)/ ``: `'/api/admin/exchange-rates/sync'` passes on the `/` branch. The count pin (`toBe(3)`, "one read, one per-currency read and one write") becomes **4** in 1e.1. **Also:** `EXCHANGE_BANS` forbids `https?://` in every non-test file under `app/admin/exchange-rates/`, so the page may not hold the ECB URL or link to it (SA-1e-5). 1e.2 adds **no** `fetch(`: Accept reuses the existing `patch` callback |
| Audit registry (SQ-47) | ✅ `EXCHANGE_RATE_*` live in `lib/audit/events.ts` (the const at 398-399, metadata at 1206-1215: `info`, `SOC2`) and in `lib/audit/eventAudience.ts` as `'shared'` (141-142). The pin is in `lib/audit/__tests__/eventAudience.test.ts:82`, "60 Business OS, 74 shared, 84 AgentsPilot", `toHaveLength(218)`, with a dated `+n` comment per change. No other test pins the total (grep `218`). `AUDIT_ENTITY_TYPES` already has `'exchange_rate'` (`lib/audit/types.ts:127`). `logAndFlush(entry, logger, { reason, continues })` is used by the PATCH |
| `PATCH` body (SQ-43, SQ-44) | ✅ `patchBodySchema = z.discriminatedUnion('field', [rate .strict(), enabled .strict()])`. The route pre-reads (`findByCode`), then makes one update, then audits `{ currencyCode, field, oldValue, newValue }`. The view's single `patch(currencyCode, body: ExchangeRatePatchBody)` callback (`ExchangeRatesView.tsx:333`) is the only PATCH site, and `saveRate` builds `{ field: 'rate', rate }`. Optional keys on the `rate` branch alone leave both existing shapes valid, and every other unknown key is still refused |
| Schema facts | ✅ `20261082`: `rate_to_usd numeric(12,6) NOT NULL`, `CHECK (rate_to_usd > 0)`, `is_enabled boolean DEFAULT true` (nullable; the wire reads null as false). A current rate is never null, so a gap always has a base, and its % never divides by zero |
| Repository | ✅ `ExchangeRatesRepository.listAll()` exists, carries the documented rule 4 exemption (global table, no owner column, admin-only, service role intentional), and is the only read the sync needs. No new method, so `adminReadMethods.guard` is unaffected |
| Finance guards | ✅ `finance.source.guard` forbids `exchange_rates` in `lib/business-os/finance/**` (lines 452-453, 948). The `app/admin/finance/**` files name the **tab** (`exchange-rates`), never the table. 1e touches neither tree |
| `console.*` in touched files | ✅ 0 in all of: the four view components, both rates routes, `exchangeRateRules.ts`, `events.ts`, `eventAudience.ts` |

#### Rulings

| # | Ruling |
|---|---|
| SQ-35 | **Approved under RR-8**, as the second feature allowed to fetch from a serverless route. The URL is exactly `https://www.ecb.europa.eu/stats/eurofxref/eurofxref-daily.xml`, a code constant in **one** file (`lib/admin/exchangeRates/ecbFeed.ts`, `import 'server-only'`). The shape is unchanged: `redirect: 'manual'` (any 3xx is `http_3xx`); `cache: 'no-store'`; only `User-Agent` and `Accept`, no cookie; a 10 s timer over headers and body; one retry on a network error or 5xx, none on a 4xx or a timeout; `text/html` and 403 are `blocked`; never throws; the body is never logged or audited. Limits for this source: `Accept: 'application/xml, text/xml'`; content types `['text/xml', 'application/xml']`; **byte cap 64 KB** (the file is about 1.8 KB; this leaves room for the ECB adding currencies, and stops anything page-sized). User-Agent: `AgentPilot-RateCheck/1.0 (admin-triggered exchange rate review; one request per click)`. This approval covers this URL for this feature only. The ECB's 90-day and historical files, Frankfurter and every other §5.7.2 source are **not** approved |
| SQ-36 | **Copy, do not lift.** Lifting the core would force edits to `fetchSources.source.test` (count 1 → 0, and its URL pin) and to a charging-adjacent, ratcheted module, in a slice that is otherwise read-only. A copy is about 100 lines, and it is the same reviewed shape. `ecbFeed.ts` copies `attempt` / `readCapped` / `discardBody` / the retry loop **as-is**. It keeps its own types (`EcbFetchResult` with the same outcome strings) and its own source guard (step 1e.1-9), and it imports **nothing** from `lib/ai/pricing-research/fetchSources.ts`. The file header names its origin ("copy of the price review's `fetchLimited`, SA §13.7 SQ-36") so a fix in one is looked for in the other. **Follow-up FU-1e-1:** when a third outbound consumer is proposed, lift both copies into one shared server-only helper in that slice, under its own SA review. `fetchSources.ts` and its tests stay **unedited** |
| SQ-37 | **ECB directly, XML, no dependency.** Frankfurter is rejected: it is a third party between us and the official source, it rounds to 5 significant digits, and it would need its own RR-8 approval anyway. There is no XML library, no `DOMParser` (absent on Node anyway), no `eval` and no `new Function`. The parser is a pure string reader with linear, anchored patterns (SA-1e-3). Without an XML parser there is no entity expansion, so no XXE or "billion laughs" risk exists; the byte cap bounds the input before any pattern runs |
| SQ-38 | **Accepted, amended: reuse `decimal.ts`, add only the division.** A new pure `lib/admin/exchangeRates/rateMath.ts` imports `parseDecimal`, `compareDecimals`, `subtractDecimals`, `absDecimal`, `signOfDecimal` and `type Decimal` from `@/lib/ai/pricing-research/decimal` (unedited; precedent `lib/repositories/types.ts`). It adds `divideHalfUp(numerator, denominator, places): Decimal`: align scales, scale the numerator by 10^(places+1), integer-divide, then round half **away from zero** on the magnitude, with the sign applied after. It also adds `toFixedPlaces(value, places): string` (exactly `places` digits, `-` for negatives, no `+`). **No `Number(`, `parseFloat`, `parseInt`, `Math.` or `toFixed` in `rateMath.ts`, `ecbRates.ts` or the sync route's arithmetic; no BigInt literal (`\d+n\b`): use `BigInt(…)` calls.** A source test pins all of this (step 1e.1-9). A zero denominator is never reached: `ecbRates` refuses a zero USD rate first (SA-1e-3); `rateMath` still throws on one, as a programming error |
| SQ-39 | **Accepted.** `POST /api/admin/exchange-rates/sync`, a new `app/api/admin/exchange-rates/sync/route.ts`. `requireAdmin` is the **first statement** (the `[currencyCode]` order, not `fetch-runs`'). Then the correlationId child logger; the body read as text (an empty body is `{}`, invalid JSON → 400); `z.object({}).strict()` → 400 `invalid_input` ("This action takes no input."). Then `listAll()` (an error → 500, **no fetch made**); fetch; parse; compute; audit; answer. A source failure is a **200** with `outcome` ≠ `ok` and no suggestions. 500 for the unexpected, in the standard error format with `details` only in development. `runtime = 'nodejs'`, `dynamic = 'force-dynamic'`, `maxDuration = 30` (two 10 s attempts plus margin). The path collision is checked above; the PATCH-on-`sync` 405 is accepted (SA-1e-6) |
| SQ-40 | **(a) Accepted: shown only, nothing stored.** No migration. The `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN` audit is the durable record of what the feed said; the accept is recorded by `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED`. (b) needs a new requirement if the user asks |
| SQ-41 | **Accepted.** Gap = suggested − current. The absolute is `toFixedPlaces(…, 6)` with an explicit `+` when positive. The % is `divideHalfUp(gap × 100, current, 2)`, signed. **Direction and colour come from `compareDecimals(suggested, current)` on the exact values, never from the rounded %**, so a gap of `+0.000001` reads `+0.00%` but is still red. No tolerance band: the user's rule (BQ-2) is "no colour when equal", and a band would reopen it. "No change" exactly when `compareDecimals` is 0, which agrees with `sameRate` at 6 decimals |
| SQ-42 | **Allowed. This is the binding reading of D-5 and the CLAUDE.md row** ("An admin-maintained `exchange_rates` table exists, but no figure may be converted or summed with it"). The rule forbids applying a rate to an **amount of money**, and adding amounts in different currencies. Two operations are allowed: (i) deriving one rate from two rates of the same official feed (ECB(X) ÷ ECB(USD)), which yields a rate, not a converted figure; (ii) the difference between two rates **of the same currency**, absolute or as a % of one of them, which is a property of the rate. Neither takes an amount as input or produces one. Conditions: the sync payload and the page carry **no** amount, total, price or USD figure; the worked money example in §5.7.5 stays in this document and is never page copy; the new modules live under `lib/admin/exchangeRates/`, never `lib/business-os/**`; and `rateMath.ts` / `ecbRates.ts` headers record this ruling in one line, as `exchangeRateRules.ts` does ("a rate is only validated, compared with another rate, and shown"). Any future use of a rate on an amount still needs a user decision reversing D-5 |
| SQ-43 | **Accepted, optional and additive, on the `rate` branch only.** `origin: z.object({ kind: z.literal('sync'), source: z.literal('ecb'), rateDate: z.string().refine(isRealDate) }).strict().optional()`. When present, the audit `details` gain `origin` exactly as received. It is page-reported, and the server trusts it for the record only: it never changes validation, the value saved or the answer. The `enabled` branch is unchanged; `origin` there is a 400 |
| SQ-44 | **Accepted, optional, on the `rate` branch only.** `expectedRate: rateStringSchema.optional()`. Accept sends the `currentRate` the sync compared against. After the pre-read, `!sameRate(previous.rate_to_usd, expectedRate)` gives **409** `{ code: 'rate_changed', error: 'This rate changed since you synced. Sync again.' }`, with no write and no audit. Without `expectedRate`, behaviour is byte-for-byte today's (manual edits never send it). The sub-second gap between the pre-read and the update is accepted (one or two admins); a compare-and-set update would need a new repository method for no real benefit |
| SQ-45 | **No new tile, accepted.** The block is the existing rates table, which already has its KPIs ("Enabled currencies", "Last rate change"). A tile that is blank until a click would be a weak KPI, and it would move the `KpiStrip.callers.pin` hashes. TL shows the user this choice in one line at the diff review (the money-page KPI-first preference); it is not a blocker |
| SQ-46 | **Rejected as proposed: no server cache.** A module-level cache on Vercel lives per instance and per cold start, so two clicks can disagree on "cached". It adds state that Jest must reset between tests, and it saves nothing worth having: about 2 KB from a CDN that already sends `max-age=300`. The guard is page-side: the button is disabled while a sync runs. No 60 s refusal (there are no stored runs to measure, and the call is admin-only). Every answer is a fresh fetch with its own `fetchedAt` |
| SQ-47 | **Accepted.** `EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN: 'EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN'` beside the other two. Metadata: `severity: 'info'`, `complianceFlags: ['SOC2']`, `description: 'Public exchange rates read for review (nothing applied)'`. Audience `'shared'` (as its siblings). The pin moves `218 → 219` and `shared 74 → 75`, with the comment `// +1 shared (EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN): Admin Exchange Rates slice 1e.1, SA §13.7 (2026-10-2x).` The entry: `entityType: 'exchange_rate'`, no `entityId`, `userId` / `actorId` = the admin, `request`. Its `details`: `{ source: 'ecb', outcome, rateDate \| null, durationMs, attempts, bytes, currencies: [{ currencyCode, currentRate, suggestedRate \| null, status }] }`. The list is bounded by the enabled currencies (at most 43). There is no body, no URL from the response and no anchor text beyond the anchor's name. It is written once for every POST that passes the admin gate and the body check (success and source failure alike), with `logAndFlush(…, { reason: 'exchange rate sync', continues: 'the suggestions are still shown' })`. An audit failure never fails the sync |
| SQ-48 | **Accepted.** The two columns render only after the first successful sync of the visit, so every existing table render test stays unedited. G-6 count `3 → 4` is a named forced edit with a comment line. `KpiStrip.callers.pin` is unchanged. The fixture holds no backslash (the PR #180 hazard) and is dated in its name |

#### Additional rulings

| # | Topic | Ruling |
|---|---|---|
| SA-1e-1 | A gap against an edited rate | The answer carries, per currency, the `currentRate` the server compared against. Suggestions survive a save and a re-read (FR-43), but a gap is only true for that base. The page therefore picks the cell state per row, by string comparison through `sameRate` (no arithmetic, no number parsing): **(1) Accepted:** the code is in the page's accepted set (1e.2, set on a successful Accept or Confirm and cleared by the next sync), **or** `!sameRate(s.currentRate, s.suggestedRate) && sameRate(row.rateToUsd, s.suggestedRate)` → "Accepted", with no button. **(2)** Else, if `sameRate(row.rateToUsd, s.currentRate)` → the gap as served (including "No change" for `direction: 'none'`), plus Accept or "Confirm (no change)" (1e.2). **(3)** Else → "Rate changed since the sync. Sync again.", with no gap colour and no button. FR-43 is read with this amendment. In 1e.1 the accepted set does not exist yet, and (1) only arises after a manual edit to the suggested value |
| SA-1e-2 | Where the button and state live | The fetch, the busy flag, the sync answer and the error live in `ExchangeRatesView` (the folder's only fetch owner). The button sits in the view's existing header actions, beside Refresh. `ExchangeRatesTable` stays presentational and gains one optional prop, `sync: RateSyncView \| null` (null → today's columns, exactly). The button is disabled while a sync runs, and while there is no successful plain read (no rows to place suggestions on). An answer that arrives after unmount is dropped (a mounted ref or an `AbortController`), as SA-1d-1 does for reads |
| SA-1e-3 | The parser (`ecbRates.ts`, pure) | `parseEcbDaily(text): { ok: true; rateDate: string; perEur: ReadonlyMap<string, string> } \| { ok: false; anchor: EcbAnchor }`. Anchors, each failure being `layout_changed` with its name. **E1:** `<gesmes:name>European Central Bank</gesmes:name>` present. **E2:** exactly one `<Cube\s+time=(['"])(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\1\s*>` and `isRealDate`. **E3:** every rate line matches `/<Cube\s+currency=(['"])([A-Z]{3})\1\s+rate=(['"])(\d{1,6}(?:\.\d{1,10})?)\3\s*\/>/g`, **and** the strict match count equals the loose `/<Cube\s+currency=/g` count, so a changed attribute order fails loudly instead of silently dropping a currency. **E4:** no code twice. **E5:** USD present with a non-zero rate. Each pattern is linear: no nested or adjacent unbounded quantifiers over the same characters, bounded digit runs, and it runs on input already capped at 64 KB. A per-currency rate of zero, or a derived rate that fails `RATE_PATTERN` / `isPositiveRate`, is that row's `no_usable_rate`, not a feed failure. `perUsdRates(perEur, codes)`: X ≠ EUR → `divideHalfUp(X, USD, 6)`; EUR → `divideHalfUp(1, USD, 6)`; USD itself is never requested; a code missing from the feed → `not_in_feed` (never 1, D-4) |
| SA-1e-4 | Wire types (`exchangeRateRules.ts`, pure) | `syncBodySchema = z.object({}).strict()`. `RateSyncOutcome = 'ok' \| 'timeout' \| 'network_error' \| 'blocked' \| 'layout_changed' \| 'too_large' \| \`http_${number}\``. `RateSuggestionStatus = 'suggested' \| 'not_in_feed' \| 'no_usable_rate'`. `GapDirection = 'up' \| 'down' \| 'none'`. `RateSuggestionWire { currencyCode; currentRate; status; suggestedRate: string \| null; gapAbsolute: string \| null; gapPercent: string \| null; direction: GapDirection \| null }`. `ExchangeRateSyncPayload { source: 'ECB'; outcome; rateDate: string \| null; fetchedAt; suggestions: RateSuggestionWire[] }`. Copy constants: `SYNC_OUTCOME_TEXT` (one plain sentence per outcome; `http_*` shares one sentence naming the status), and the §5.7.5 direction words. All strings are built on the server or in constants; the page prints them |
| SA-1e-5 | No URL on the page | G-6 bans `https?://` in `app/admin/exchange-rates/**`. The source shows as the text "ECB" (the attribution "Source: ECB" meets the ECB's reuse terms). No link to the ECB is shown, and the URL lives only in `ecbFeed.ts`. The fetch time is printed by slicing the ISO string (`HH:MM UTC`), not by locale formatting, so tests are deterministic |
| SA-1e-6 | `PATCH /api/admin/exchange-rates/sync` | It becomes 405 instead of 400 once `sync/route.ts` exists (static wins). Accepted; it needs no test edit. Dev confirms in the handover that no existing test sends it |
| SA-1e-7 | Skills and domains | Walk the `new-api-route` checklist for the sync route (and the PATCH in 1e.2): `requireAdmin` first, Zod before any read, correlationId, error format, `logAndFlush`. Not triggered: **no** entitlements import (type-only included), **no** LLM call (and none may read the feed, as RR-1), **no** cron or queue (D-9: a click), **no** caller-supplied tenant or id. The feed, the currency list and the rates all come from the server; the table is global with the documented rule 4 exemption. Schema facts are verified from `20261082` (above). Pino `createLogger({ module: 'AdminExchangeRatesSync' })` with a correlationId child, one line per sync: outcome, status, duration, attempts, bytes, currency count. The new files have 0 `console.*` |
| SA-1e-8 | Cycle | The step lists below are the workplan; deviations are recorded as DV-n in a short workplan MD that TL opens for QA. Under the user's exchange-rates fast-track, TL decides whether the user sees the diff. **SA recommends that the user sees the 1e.1 diff**: it is the platform's first outbound host outside the price review. That is a recommendation, not a gate |

#### Rulings

| # | Ruling |
|---|---|
| SQ-49 | **Accepted, amended.** (1) **The link is a reasonable design, not a loophole, and G-6 stays unedited.** G-6's `https?://` ban is a text proxy for "the page never contacts an outside host". The binding rule is `ALLOWED_FETCH`, which still refuses any `fetch(` whose first argument is not a `/api/admin/exchange-rates` literal, so the imported constant cannot be fetched. When an admin clicks a link, the browser navigates to the page; the page itself contacts no outside host. **No allow-list in G-6:** an allow-list would let `https://` text into the folder, which is weaker than today. (2) **Location:** `ECB_SOURCE_LABEL = 'European Central Bank (euro foreign exchange reference rates)'`, `ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL` (the BA's URL, verified above) and `ECB_SOURCE_NOTE = 'Published by the ECB for information only.'` live in the **new** pure `lib/admin/exchangeRates/rateSyncRules.ts` (SQ-52), not in `exchangeRateRules.ts`. (3) **`rateSync.source.test`:** exactly **two** `https://` literals across `lib/admin/exchangeRates/**` non-test files: `ECB_DAILY_URL` in `ecbFeed.ts` and `ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL` in `rateSyncRules.ts`, each pinned to its exact value. Zero in `exchangeRateRules.ts`. In `app/admin/exchange-rates/**`, `ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL` appears **once**, as `href={ECB_SOURCE_PAGE_URL}`. Neither `ECB_DAILY_URL` nor `ecbFeed` is imported anywhere under `app/admin/`. Each rule has a planted control. The file header cites this ruling, so G-6's silence on links is on record. (4) **What the admin sees:** one always-visible line beside Sync rates: "Source: " + the label as a link, then `ECB_SOURCE_NOTE`. The link has `target="_blank"`, `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, an `aria-hidden` `ExternalLink` icon and an `sr-only` " (opens in a new tab)" (BoostBlock precedent). **No header tooltip** (FR-50 amended): the user asked for "tool tip or text near it", text near it meets that, and a header tooltip would be a new pattern for the same words. The per-cell "ECB · rates of … · fetched … UTC" line is unchanged |
| SQ-50 | **(a) Accepted, amended.** `EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED`: `severity: 'info'`, `complianceFlags: ['SOC2']`, `description: 'Suggested exchange rate accepted by an admin'`, audience `'shared'`. The `PATCH` writes it **instead of** `EXCHANGE_RATE_UPDATED` when the `rate` save carries `origin`: one save, one entry. A manual edit, a 400, a 404 and a 409 are unchanged (no new entry). **Amendments:** (1) **`details` is a superset of the sibling's shape**, so anything that reads rate changes reads both events the same way: `{ currencyCode, field: 'rate', oldValue, newValue, unchanged, origin: { kind, source, rateDate }, gap: { gapAbsolute, gapPercent, direction } }`. The BA's `oldRate` is `oldValue` (the pre-read, as today), and `suggestedRate` is `newValue` (the **stored** value, not the page's string). `gap` is `rateGap(oldValue, newValue)` on the server; nothing in it comes from the page. `origin` is page-reported and kept for the record only (SQ-43). (2) **`origin` requires `expectedRate`:** on the `rate` branch, a `.superRefine` makes `origin` without `expectedRate` a 400. Then every accept entry was checked against the rate the sync compared (SQ-44), and an accept can never be recorded against a base that had changed. (3) "Confirm (no change)" uses the same event (`unchanged: true`, direction `none`). (4) The pin becomes **220 / bos 60 / shared 76 / agentspilot 84**, with one comment line: `// +2 shared (EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN, EXCHANGE_RATE_SUGGESTION_ACCEPTED): Admin Exchange Rates slice 1e, SA §13.7 SQ-47 / §13.8 SQ-50 (2026-10-2x).` (5) `filterOptions.guard.test.ts` and `filterOptions.test.ts` are **not edited** (verified above) |
| SQ-51 | **Accepted, amended: read the audit, no migration, no index.** (1) **Repository:** `AuditTrailRepository.findLatestAdminExchangeRateSync(context: AdminAuditReadContext): Promise<RepositoryResult<AdminExchangeRateSyncRun \| null>>`. It refuses a missing context before any query. The query is `.from('audit_trail').select(ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_COLUMNS)` with `ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_COLUMNS = 'created_at, rate_date:details->>rateDate'` (exported, no `details` blob, no actor, no ip), then `.eq('action', AUDIT_EVENTS.EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_RUN)`, `.eq('entity_type', 'exchange_rate')`, `.eq('details->>outcome', 'ok')`, `.order('created_at', { ascending: false })`, `.limit(1)`. A `rate_date` that fails `isRealDate` is an error, not a row. It logs info with `rows` only, and warns on failure. The header gets "THE SIXTH ADMIN EXCEPTION": not `user_id`-scoped, because it is a platform event whose user id is the acting admin. Live audit only. Its only caller is the plain `GET`. Add the name to `ADMIN_METHODS`, with a comment line. (2) **Index:** none needed (see the table). If the read is ever slow, the tile goes `unavailable` and the page still works. (3) **The `GET`:** the read is the **fourth member of the existing `Promise.all`**, so it adds no latency. It is wrapped in a local `settleLastSync()` that turns a returned error **or a throw** into `{ status: 'unavailable' }` and logs `warn` with `failed: 'lastSync'`. It **never** gives a 500; the header records this as the one exception to "every read must succeed". The `?currency=` read never calls it, and a test pins that. (4) **Wire, three states**, which `\| null` cannot express (the BA's own FR-55 needs "unavailable"): `LastRateSyncWire = { status: 'ok'; checkedAt: string; rateDate: string } \| { status: 'none' } \| { status: 'unavailable' }`. The `GET` answer type is `ExchangeRatesReadPayload = ExchangeRatesPayload & { lastSync: LastRateSyncWire }`, both in `rateSyncRules.ts`. `ExchangeRatesPayload` itself is unchanged (SQ-52). (5) **What feeds the tile:** after a successful sync, the **sync answer** feeds it. No re-`GET`, and no new field on the sync answer: a pure `lastSyncFromSync(answer)` gives `{ status: 'ok', checkedAt: fetchedAt, rateDate }` when `outcome === 'ok'`, else `null`. The view keeps the visit's value and shows it ahead of the `GET`'s, so a later re-read cannot move the tile back even if that sync's audit flush failed. A failed sync leaves the tile unchanged. (6) **Tile:** `testId="kpi-rates-last-checked"`, label "Rates last checked", value `formatUtc(checkedAt)`, sub "ECB rates of {rateDate}". For `none`: "No sync on record", because audit archiving can remove an old entry and "Never" would then be false. For `unavailable`: `status="unavailable"` (the existing "Could not check"), no value, sub "The audit trail could not be read". It goes after the two existing tiles, which are unchanged, and the loading strip shows 3 placeholders. `ExchangeRatesKpis` gains a required prop `lastSync: LastRateSyncWire` (the view passes the visit's value or the `GET`'s). (7) **`KpiStrip.callers.pin`:** the five Exchange rates cases are re-hashed: loading has 3 placeholders, and the four payload cases gain `lastSync` ok. Two cases are appended (`none`, `unavailable`). A dated amendment line is added. No other hash moves. **SQ-48's "unchanged" is superseded** |
| SQ-52 | **Accepted: one branch, one PR, seven commits** (plan below). (1) **New module `lib/admin/exchangeRates/rateSyncRules.ts`** (pure, no `server-only`, imported by the page) holds the whole sync contract: the SA-1e-4 types and `syncBodySchema`, `SYNC_SOURCE_LABEL`, `SYNC_OUTCOME_TEXT`, the direction words, the three ECB source constants, `LastRateSyncWire`, `ExchangeRatesReadPayload` and `lastSyncFromSync`. It imports only from `exchangeRateRules.ts` (existing exports and the type `ExchangeRatesPayload`), never the other way round, so there is no import cycle. **Why:** `exchangeRateRules.ts` would otherwise be edited for three unrelated reasons (sync types, the `GET` payload, the `PATCH` schema), and the RM cannot split one file across commits. Interactive staging (`git add -p`) is not available, and hand-built partial files carry the known agent file-write truncation hazard. It also keeps the 346-line rules module about stored rates. **`exchangeRateRules.ts` now changes for the `PATCH` alone** (`expectedRate`, `rateOriginSchema`, the `superRefine`). (2) **Commits are file-disjoint**: every changed file belongs to exactly one commit. (3) **Each commit must be green on its own: required, not just recommended.** "Green" means the narrow Jest set for its paths plus the cross-cutting guards, and narrow `tsc` on its files and their importers. The PR merges with a merge commit, so its commits land on `main` and a red one would mislead a later `git bisect`. The plan below is built so this holds; the type coupling (ts-jest type-checks every test) was traced file by file. (4) The RM writes the plan into the workplan before the first commit. The **workplan is renamed** `docs/workplans/ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATES_SLICE_1E_WORKPLAN.md` (TL or Dev; one file for the whole slice) |

**How the RM proves "green on its own"** (no stash, no interactive rebase, no worktree removal):
1. Create C1 to C7 by staging each commit's exact file list.
2. With a clean tree, for each commit run `git checkout --detach <sha>`, then that commit's narrow Jest and narrow `tsc`. The commands go in the workplan.
3. Return with `git checkout feature/admin-exchange-rates-sync`.

If a commit fails, stop before the push and send it back to Dev. The commits may then be rebuilt with `git reset --soft <base>` and restaged; nothing has been pushed at that point. Before the first commit, run `git diff --stat`: every changed file must appear in exactly one row above, and the RM must stop on any file that does not. The results go in the PR body.

#### Reviews and the user's diff

- **One combined SA code review replaces the two.** It covers the whole working-tree diff before anything is committed, walked commit group by commit group in the plan's order, in one report in the workplan. C3 (the RR-8 outbound surface; the `new-api-route` checklist) and C5 (the `PATCH` change) get the line-by-line pass. C4 gets the admin-exception check. **One QA pass** covers the whole slice.
- **SA-1e-8 now covers the whole PR, and it is a gate, not a recommendation.** The user asked to see the diff and click through it before any commit. That instruction outranks the exchange-rates fast-track for this slice. The order is: Dev → SA code review → the user sees the full diff and clicks through (local dev) → QA → user approval → RM commits C1 to C7, proves each green, opens the PR. Once the PR is open, CI is the only remaining gate, so auto-merge with `--merge` is the TL's call under the standing preference.
- **The click-through list** (TL hands it to the user):
  - Sync rates, then the three suggestions and their gap colours.
  - The source link opens the ECB page in a new tab.
  - Accept one rate and Confirm another.
  - The tile updates after the sync and is still there after a reload.
  - The Audit Trail page's Action filter finds both new events.
  - The live sync makes one ECB request per click from local dev, which is allowed (SQ-35).

### 13.9 SA review: slice 1f

**Reviewed by SA — 2026-10-25**, read from `origin/main` `e0d64fb2` (1e merged, #511).
**Verdict: CLEARED-WITH-CHANGES.** D-11 is a small, read-only change on paths 1e already built. The one real choice is how the name reaches the tile right after a sync (SQ-55). Dev builds from the step list below. There is no separate workplan review.

#### Rulings

**SQ-53 Data: add `actor_id`, as `checked_by`.**
- The acting admin is in `audit_trail.actor_id`. `AuditTrailService` writes `actor_id = input.actorId || input.userId || systemAdminId` (`lib/services/AuditTrailService.ts:212`), so `actor_id` is the "who did it" column. `user_id` on a platform event is only the scope field; here it happens to be the same admin. The sync route sets both (`userId: adminId, actorId: adminId`), so every 1e sync entry holds the actor. The price review's sibling read (`ADMIN_PRICING_SYNC_RUN_COLUMNS`) also selects `actor_id`.
- `ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATE_SYNC_COLUMNS` becomes exactly `'created_at, rate_date:details->>rateDate, checked_by:actor_id'`. Still no `details` blob, no hash, no email, no ip and no `user_id`.
- Row mapping: a string → `checkedBy`; `null` → `checkedBy: null` (shown as "Unknown user"; not an error, since an old or system entry may have no actor); any other type → the method's existing error path, like a bad rate date. Filters, order, limit and the admin read context are unchanged. It is still the sixth admin exception; no new method.
- Schema: `actor_id` is an existing column, already read in production by `AUDIT_COLUMNS` and `ADMIN_PRICING_SYNC_RUN_COLUMNS`. No further schema check is needed.
- The header comment's "(the time and the feed's rate date: no `details` blob, no actor, no hash, no email, no ip)" becomes "(the time, the feed's rate date and the acting admin's id: no `details` blob, no hash, no email, no ip)". The constant's doc comment says the same.

**SQ-54 Name resolution: one lookup, extended.**
- Change `resolveLookup(records, logger)` to `resolveLookup(ids, logger)`. The plain read passes the distinct union of the history editors, the `lastChange` editor, `lastSync.checkedBy` (when `ok` and not null) and the caller's id (SQ-55). The `?currency=` read passes `historyEditorIds(records)` only, as today.
- Add a pure `editorLabel(id, lookup): string | null` in `exchangeRateRules.ts`: no id → `UNKNOWN_USER_LABEL`; failed lookup → `null`; otherwise the name, then the email, then "Unknown user". `labelHistory` is rewritten on top of it with no change in behaviour; its tests stay unedited.
- Keep the record/wire split. `settleLastSync` returns a `LastRateSyncRecord` (its `ok` case carries `checkedBy` and no label). The pure `labelLastSync(record, lookup)` in `rateSyncRules.ts` turns it into the wire `LastRateSyncWire`, whose `ok` case gains `checkedBy: string | null` and `checkedByLabel: string | null`.
- A failed lookup shows the **short id**: yes, as `changedByText` already does (FR-19, SA Q-1: "we could not look it up" is not "nobody"). `nameLookup` keeps its meaning and now also covers the sync actor and the caller.
- Knock-on: the plain read now always runs the lookup, because the caller's id is always in it. That costs two small `.in()` reads in the rare case of no history and no sync. The `?currency=` read's "no lookup at all" cases are unchanged.

**SQ-55 After a sync: (a′), the plain `GET` names the caller. (a), (b) and (c) are rejected.**
- **(a) The sync route resolves the name.** Allowed: `adminReadMethods.guard` permits any caller under `app/api/admin/**`. Rejected all the same. `resolveLookup` is module-private, because a `route.ts` may export only handlers, so this would mean copying the lookup or moving it to a new shared module. It would also add two reads and a new failure path to every sync, for a name the page can already have.
- **(b) Re-read the plain `GET` after a sync.** Rejected. It breaks the 1e rule that the visit's sync answer outranks the `GET` (SQ-51 (5)). The audit flush is bounded and may not have landed, so the re-read can return the previous sync and its actor, and the tile would then name the wrong admin.
- **(c) "by you" until reload.** Rejected. A reload then shows a name, so the same sync would read two ways.
- **Ruled (a′).** The plain `GET` adds the caller's id (`gate.user.id`) to its one lookup and returns `viewer: RateViewerWire = { userId, label }`, with `label` from `editorLabel` (null when the lookup failed). The view keeps `viewerRef` from the newest successful plain read. `lastSyncFromSync(answer, viewer)` sets `checkedBy: viewer.userId` and `checkedByLabel: viewer.label`. The admin who posts to `/sync` is the signed-in admin of the page, who is the viewer: both requests pass `requireAdmin` on the same session. So the tile right after a sync reads exactly what a reload will show. No new `fetch(` (G-6 stays 4), and the sync route is untouched. The Sync button stays disabled until a successful plain read (SA-1e-2), so the viewer is always known. For the unreachable `viewer === null` case, `checkedBy` and `checkedByLabel` are both null, which shows "Unknown user".

**SQ-56 Display.**
- The `ok` sub-line becomes `` `by ${changedByText({ changedBy: lastSync.checkedBy, changedByLabel: lastSync.checkedByLabel })} · ECB rates of ${lastSync.rateDate}` ``, for example "by Anna Admin · ECB rates of 2026-10-23". `changedByText` is reused unchanged; only its header comment gains the third tile.
- The separator is " · ", not ", ". On this tile the value is already the time; on "Last rate change" the time sits in the sub-line after the comma.
- The value, label, `testId`, "No sync on record" and "Could not check" are unchanged.
- An answer from before 1f (deploy skew) has an `ok` state with no `checkedBy`. Dev maps a missing field with `?? null`, which gives "Unknown user". There is no other special code.

**SQ-57 Pins, guards and logging.**
- **`KpiStrip.callers.pin`.** `FX_LAST_SYNC_OK` gains `checkedBy` (the fixture admin's id) and `checkedByLabel: 'Anna Admin'`.
  - **Re-hashed (4):** `two tiles`, `no history`, `name lookup failed`, `old rate null`.
  - **Unchanged (3):** `loading` (with no payload only placeholders render, and `lastSync` is never drawn), `last sync none` and `last sync unavailable`.
  - **Appended (2):** `last sync checker label failed` (label null → short id) and `last sync no checker` (`checkedBy` null → "Unknown user").
  - That makes **9 Exchange rates hashes**. No other caller is touched. Add a dated amendment line. If the `loading` hash moves, stop and report: it would mean the loading path draws `lastSync`.
- **`rateSync.source.test`:** no edit. The sync route, `ecbFeed.ts` and both URLs are untouched; it stays green.
- **`redundantPages.source.guard` (G-6):** no edit; still 4 `fetch(` call sites.
- **`adminReadMethods.guard`:** no edit. There is no new method, and the callers have not changed.
- **`AuditTrailRepository.latestExchangeRateSync.test.ts`:** forced edits. The exact-select assertion takes the new string. The "never selects …" regex drops `actor` and keeps `details`, `email`, `ip_address` and `hash`; add `user_id` to it. New cases: a null actor gives `checkedBy: null`; a non-string actor gives an error.
- **Logging:** no name and no email in any log line. Do not add `checkedBy` to any log either; there is no need, and the request logger already carries `adminId`. The `resolveLookup` debug line keeps counts only. Extend the SA-1c-7 "no name and no email reaches any log line" test with the sync actor's and the caller's name and email.

**SQ-58 Migration, commits and cycle.**
- **No migration.** `actor_id` exists and is set on every 1e sync entry. No index is needed: the read is the same one-row read on the existing filters.
- **One PR, two commits**, on `feature/admin-exchange-rates-last-check-by`:
  - **C1** `feat(admin): name who ran the last exchange rate check`: all code and tests (steps 1 to 8). The code cannot split further. The `LastRateSyncWire` shape and the `lastSyncFromSync` signature cross the server and the page, so a server-only commit would leave the page red.
  - **C2** `docs(admin): record exchange rates slice 1f`: the requirement (§1 row 1f, the SA code review and QA results in this section) and the short workplan (step 10).
- **Cycle: the exchange-rates fast-track applies.** SA code review and QA approve, then the RM commits, opens the PR and turns on auto-merge (`--merge`), with no wait for the user to see the diff. The conditions hold: no business question, no prod migration, and CI is the only gate. SA-1e-8's user-diff gate was for 1e only and does not carry over.

#### Domain checks

- No entitlements import (type-only included), no LLM call, no cron or queue.
- No caller-supplied id. The actor comes from the audit row; the viewer comes from the session gate.
- `requireAdmin` stays the first statement. The unscoped platform read, the sixth exception, is unchanged in scope.
- Untouched: the sync route, `app/admin/finance/**`, `lib/business-os/**`, the audit registry and `eventAudience`.
- Every file in the diff is already Pino-only (0 `console.*` checked in the route, both rules modules, the repository, the view, the KPIs and `changedByText`).

#### Dev step list

1. **`lib/admin/exchangeRates/exchangeRateRules.ts`.** Add `export function editorLabel(id: string | null, lookup: EditorLookup): string | null` (SQ-54) and rewrite `labelHistory` to use it, with no change in behaviour. **Test** (`__tests__/exchangeRateRules.test.ts`): `editorLabel` for no id, a failed lookup, name, blank name falling back to email, and neither. The existing `labelHistory` tests stay unedited and green.
2. **`lib/admin/exchangeRates/rateSyncRules.ts`.**
   - `LastRateSyncWire`'s `ok` case gains `checkedBy: string | null; checkedByLabel: string | null`.
   - Add `LastRateSyncRecord`: the same three states, with `ok` carrying `checkedBy` and no label.
   - Add `RateViewerWire = { userId: string; label: string | null }`.
   - `ExchangeRatesReadPayload = ExchangeRatesPayload & { lastSync: LastRateSyncWire; viewer: RateViewerWire }`.
   - Add `labelLastSync(record, lookup): LastRateSyncWire`. `none` and `unavailable` pass through; `ok` gets `checkedByLabel: editorLabel(record.checkedBy, lookup)`.
   - Change to `lastSyncFromSync(answer, viewer: RateViewerWire | null)`, with `checkedBy: viewer?.userId ?? null` and `checkedByLabel: viewer?.label ?? null`. A failed sync still gives `null`.
   - Header comment: add a line for 1f.
   - **Test** (`rateSyncRules.test.ts`): `labelLastSync` for all three states, a failed lookup (label null) and a null actor ("Unknown user"); `lastSyncFromSync` with a viewer, a viewer whose label is null, and `null`.
3. **`lib/repositories/AuditTrailRepository.ts`.** Make the constant change, the mapping and the two comment edits of SQ-53. Add `checkedBy: string | null` to `AdminExchangeRateSyncRun`. **Test:** the forced edits and new cases of SQ-57.
4. **`app/api/admin/exchange-rates/route.ts`.**
   - `resolveLookup(ids, logger)`: the early return for zero ids stays.
   - `settleLastSync` returns `LastRateSyncRecord` with `checkedBy` from the repository.
   - The plain read builds its ids as `[...new Set([...historyEditorIds(allRecords), ...(lastSync.status === 'ok' && lastSync.checkedBy ? [lastSync.checkedBy] : []), gate.user.id])]`, where `allRecords` is the windowed rows plus `latestRecord`.
   - The payload gets `lastSync: labelLastSync(lastSyncRecord, lookup)` and `viewer: { userId: gate.user.id, label: editorLabel(gate.user.id, lookup) }`.
   - The `?currency=` branch passes `historyEditorIds(records)`.
   - Header: the "Changed by" paragraph names the sync actor and the caller as the other ids in the one lookup, never taken from the request.
   - Logs unchanged.
5. **Route test** (`app/api/admin/exchange-rates/__tests__/route.test.ts`).
   - Forced edits: the happy-path body (`lastSync.checkedBy` and `checkedByLabel`, `viewer`); "the ids looked up come from …" (now also the sync actor and the caller, still never from the request); "no history at all: … nothing is looked up" (now only the caller is looked up); "only null editors: no lookup" if it is a plain-read case (the caller is then looked up); the exact-keys test (`viewer` and the two `ok` keys).
   - New cases: the sync actor is named through the same single call to each source; a sync actor who is also a history editor is looked up once; a failed lookup nulls the `lastSync` label, the `viewer` label, every history label and `lastChange` together, and still answers 200; `lastSync` `none` or `unavailable` still gets a `viewer`; a null actor gives "Unknown user".
   - Extend the SA-1c-7 log test (SQ-57).
   - The `?currency=` cases stay unedited.
6. **`app/admin/exchange-rates/components/ExchangeRatesKpis.tsx`.** Make the SQ-56 sub-line change and add a line to the header comment. **`changedByText.ts`:** comment only.
7. **`app/admin/exchange-rates/components/ExchangeRatesView.tsx`.**
   - Add `viewerRef = useRef<RateViewerWire | null>(null)`, set beside `setPayload(result.data)` in `load`, only for the current request.
   - `runSync` calls `lastSyncFromSync(answer, viewerRef.current)`, so its deps stay empty.
   - The visit-ahead-of-`GET` rule is unchanged.
   - No new `fetch(`. Add a line for 1f to the header comment.
8. **Tests.**
   - **Render test** (`app/admin/exchange-rates/__tests__/exchangeRates.render.test.tsx`): the `payload()` and `syncPagePayload()` fixtures gain `viewer` and the `ok` checker fields (a forced edit). New cases:
     - after a reload, "by Anna Admin · ECB rates of …";
     - a failed label shows the short id;
     - no actor shows "Unknown user";
     - after a sync, the tile names the viewer's label with no second `GET` (count the `GET` calls);
     - after a sync, a later re-read carrying an older `lastSync` from another admin does not replace the visit's name.
   - **`KpiStrip.callers.pin`:** as SQ-57.
9. **Checks.**
   - Run the touched suites: `npm test -- lib/admin/exchangeRates lib/repositories/__tests__/AuditTrailRepository.latestExchangeRateSync.test.ts lib/repositories/__tests__/adminReadMethods.guard.test.ts app/api/admin/exchange-rates app/admin/exchange-rates app/admin/components/layout/__tests__/KpiStrip.callers.pin.render.test.tsx app/admin/__tests__/redundantPages.source.guard.test.ts`.
   - Run the typecheck, filtered to the touched files, and confirm the command finished (no OOM).
   - Run `npm run lint` and `npm run build`.
   - Then `git diff --stat`, and leave everything uncommitted.
10. **Docs** (C2). Write a short `docs/workplans/ADMIN_EXCHANGE_RATES_SLICE_1F_WORKPLAN.md` that points to this step list, with `## SA Review Notes` and `## QA` sections. Record the build status in §1 row 1f. The RM adds the PR number.
